```
    21→
    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    27→    if not dim_scores:
    28→        return []
    29→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    43→    return f"T{tier}"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    59→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    60→            "dim",
    61→        )
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    93→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    94→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    95→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    96→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    97→}
    98→
    99→
   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   105→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   106→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   107→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   108→
   109→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   110→    members = item.get("members", [])
   111→    if members:
   112→        # File distribution
   113→        from collections import Counter
   114→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   115→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   116→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   117→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   118→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   119→        else:
   120→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   121→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   122→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   123→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   124→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   125→
   126→        # Sample IDs
   127→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   128→        for m in members[:3]:
   129→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   130→        if len(members) > 3:
   131→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   132→
   133→    # Action
   134→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   135→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   136→    if primary_command:
   137→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   138→
   139→    # Resolution hints
   140→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" '
   141→                   '--note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   142→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   143→                   "dim"))
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _render_item(
   148→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   149→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   152→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   153→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   157→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   158→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   159→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   160→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   161→
   162→    # Effort/type label
   163→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   164→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   165→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   166→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   169→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   170→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   171→        subjective_score = float(
   172→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   173→        )
   174→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   175→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   179→                "cyan",
   180→            )
   181→        )
   182→        print(colorize(
   183→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   184→            "dim",
   185→        ))
   186→        if explain:
   187→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   188→                "policy",
   189→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   190→            )
   191→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   195→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   196→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   197→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   198→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   199→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   200→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   201→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   202→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   203→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   204→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   205→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   206→
   207→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   208→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   211→    if detail.get("lines"):
   212→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   213→    if detail.get("category"):
   214→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   215→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   216→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   217→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   218→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   219→
   220→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   221→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   222→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   223→        if snippet:
   224→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   225→            print(snippet)
   226→
   227→    if dim_scores:
   228→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   229→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   230→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   231→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   232→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   233→            print(
   234→                colorize(
   235→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   236→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   237→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   238→                    "dim",
   239→                )
   240→            )
   241→
   242→    # Score impact estimate
   243→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   244→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   245→        try:
   246→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   247→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   248→            if impact > 0:
   249→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   250→            else:
   251→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   252→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   253→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   254→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   255→                    if issues > 1:
   256→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   257→                        if bulk > 0:
   258→                            print(colorize(
   259→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   260→                                "cyan",
   261→                            ))
   262→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   263→            pass
   264→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   265→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   266→        try:
   267→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   268→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   269→            if dim_key:
   270→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   271→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   272→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   273→                        continue
   274→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   275→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   276→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   277→                        if drag > 0.01:
   278→                            print(colorize(
   279→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   280→                                "cyan",
   281→                            ))
   282→                        break
   283→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   284→            pass
   285→
   286→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   287→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   288→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   289→        similar_count = sum(
   290→            1
   291→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   292→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   293→        )
   294→        if similar_count > 1:
   295→            print(
   296→                colorize(
   297→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   298→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   299→                    "cyan",
   300→                )
   301→            )
   302→    if explain:
   303→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   304→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   305→        base = (
   306→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   307→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   308→        )
   309→
   310→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   311→        if dim_scores and detector:
   312→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   313→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   314→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   315→                base += (
   316→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   317→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   318→                )
   319→
   320→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   321→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   322→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   323→            if dim_key:
   324→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   325→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   326→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   327→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   328→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   329→                        break
   330→
   331→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   332→        if policy:
   333→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   334→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True
   364→
   365→
   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→
```

```
1→"""Canonical detector registry — single source of truth.
     2→
     3→All detector metadata lives here. Other modules derive their views
     4→(display order, CLI names, narrative tools, scoring validation) from this registry
     5→instead of maintaining their own lists.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from collections.abc import Callable
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→DISPLAY_ORDER = [
    14→    "logs",
    15→    "unused",
    16→    "exports",

   322→
   323→_DISPLAY_ORDER = list(DISPLAY_ORDER)
   324→
   325→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```

```
     2→
     3→Separate module to avoid import cycles — submodules import from here
     4→instead of from __init__.py.
     5→"""

     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import (
    10→    detector_tools as _detector_tools,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.core.registry import (
    13→    on_detector_registered,
    14→)
    15→
    16→DETECTOR_TOOLS = _detector_tools()
    17→
    18→
    19→def _refresh_detector_tools() -> None:
    20→    """Rebuild DETECTOR_TOOLS from current DETECTORS."""
    21→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.clear()
    22→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.update(_detector_tools())
    23→
    24→
    25→# Auto-refresh when new detectors are registered at runtime.
    26→on_detector_registered(_refresh_detector_tools)
```

```
1→"""Tool inventory helpers for narrative payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_engine import supported_fixers
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ToolFixer, ToolInventory
    10→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    11→
    12→
    13→def _move_reasons(by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> list[str]:
    14→    reasons: list[str] = []
    15→    if by_detector.get("orphaned", 0):
    16→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['orphaned']} orphaned files")
    17→    if by_detector.get("coupling", 0):
    18→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['coupling']} coupling violations")
    19→    if by_detector.get("single_use", 0):
    20→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['single_use']} single-use files")
    21→    if by_detector.get("flat_dirs", 0):
    22→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['flat_dirs']} flat directories")
    23→    if by_detector.get("naming", 0):
    24→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['naming']} naming issues")
    25→    return reasons
    26→
    27→
    28→def _build_fixers(
    29→    by_detector: dict[str, int], state: StateModel, lang: str | None
    30→) -> list[ToolFixer]:
    31→    fixers: list[ToolFixer] = []
    32→    supported = supported_fixers(state, lang)
    33→
    34→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    35→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
    36→            continue
    37→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
    38→        if count == 0:
    39→            continue
    40→
    41→        for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]:
    42→            if supported is not None and fixer not in supported:
    43→                continue
    44→            fixers.append(
    45→                {
    46→                    "name": fixer,
    47→                    "detector": detector,
    48→                    "open_count": count,
    49→                    "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
    50→                }
    51→            )
    52→
    53→    return fixers
    54→
    55→
    56→def compute_tools(
    57→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    58→    state: StateModel,
    59→    lang: str | None,
    60→    badge: dict[str, Any],
    61→) -> ToolInventory:
    62→    move_keys = ["orphaned", "flat_dirs", "naming", "single_use", "coupling", "cycles"]
    63→    organizational_issues = sum(by_detector.get(detector, 0) for detector in move_keys)
    64→    reasons = _move_reasons(by_detector)
    65→
    66→    return {
    67→        "fixers": _build_fixers(by_detector, state, lang),
    68→        "move": {
    69→            "available": True,
    70→            "relevant": organizational_issues > 0,
    71→            "reason": " + ".join(reasons) if reasons else None,
    72→            "usage": "desloppify move <source> <dest> [--dry-run]",
    73→        },
    74→        "plan": {
    75→            "command": "desloppify plan",
    76→            "description": "Generate prioritized markdown cleanup plan",
    77→        },
    78→        "badge": badge,
    79→    }
    80→
```

```
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(

    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    89→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    90→
    91→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    # Load the living plan
    94→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    95→    plan = load_plan()
    96→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    97→    if (
    98→        plan.get("queue_order")
    99→        or plan.get("overrides")
   100→        or plan.get("clusters")
   101→    ):
   102→        plan_data = plan
   103→
   104→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   105→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   106→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   107→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   108→        if active_cluster:
   109→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   110→
   111→    queue = build_work_queue(
   112→        state,
   113→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   114→            tier=tier,
   115→            count=count,
   116→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   117→            scope=scope,
   118→            status=status,
   119→            include_subjective=True,
   120→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   121→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   122→            explain=explain,
   123→            plan=plan_data,
   124→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   125→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   126→        ),
   127→    )
   128→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   131→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   132→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   133→        state,
   134→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   138→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   139→    )
   140→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   141→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   142→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   143→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   144→        state,
   145→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   146→    )
   147→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   148→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   149→    ]
   150→    write_query(payload)
   151→
   152→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   153→    if output_file:
   154→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   155→            output_file,
   156→            payload,
   157→            len(items),
   158→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   159→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   160→        ):
   161→            return
   162→        raise SystemExit(1)
   163→
   164→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   168→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   169→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   170→        return
   171→
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   173→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   174→        state.get("findings", {}),
   175→        state.get("scan_path"),
   176→    )
   177→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   178→    try:
   179→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   180→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   181→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   182→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   183→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   184→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   185→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   186→    )
   187→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   188→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   189→        state,
   190→        dim_scores,
   191→        findings_scoped,
   192→        strict_score=strict_score,
   193→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   194→    )
   195→    print()
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   199→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    43→
    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        meta_item: dict = {
   231→            "id": cname,
   232→            "kind": "cluster",
   233→            "action_type": action_type,
   234→            "summary": summary,
   235→            "members": members,
   236→            "member_count": len(members),
   237→            "primary_command": cluster_data.get("action"),
   238→            "cluster_name": cname,
   239→            "cluster_auto": True,
   240→            "confidence": "high",
   241→            "detector": detector,
   242→            "file": "",
   243→        }
   244→        result.append(meta_item)
   245→
   246→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   247→    return result
   248→
```

```
250→def build_work_queue(
   251→    state: StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   254→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   255→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   256→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   257→
   258→    status = resolved_options.status
   259→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   260→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   261→    try:
   262→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   263→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   264→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   265→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   266→
   267→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   268→        state,
   269→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   270→        status_filter=status,
   271→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   272→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   273→    )
   274→
   275→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   276→    if (
   277→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   278→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   279→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   280→    ):
   281→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   282→            state,
   283→            state.get("findings", {}),
   284→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   285→        )
   286→        for item in subjective_items:
   287→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   288→                all_items.append(item)
   289→
   290→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   291→
   292→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   293→    if resolved_options.plan:
   294→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   295→            all_items,
   296→            resolved_options.plan,
   297→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   298→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   299→        )
   300→
   301→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   302→    should_collapse = (
   303→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   304→        and resolved_options.plan
   305→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   306→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   307→    )
   308→    if should_collapse:
   309→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   310→
   311→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   312→
   313→    requested_tier = (
   314→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   315→    )
   316→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   317→    fallback_reason = None
   318→    filtered = all_items
   319→
   320→    if requested_tier is not None:
   321→        filtered = [
   322→            item
   323→            for item in all_items
   324→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   325→        ]
   326→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   327→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   328→            if chosen is not None:
   329→                selected_tier = chosen
   330→                filtered = [
   331→                    item
   332→                    for item in all_items
   333→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   334→                ]
   335→                fallback_reason = (
   336→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   337→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   338→                )
   339→        elif not filtered:
   340→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   341→
   342→    total = len(filtered)
   343→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   344→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   345→
   346→    if resolved_options.explain:
   347→        for item in filtered:
   348→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   349→
```

```
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )

   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
```

## Comprehensive Report: Narrative/Coaching System & Next Command Integration

### 1. **Narrative System Architecture (`compute_narrative()` Orchestrator)**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/core.py` (lines 314–387)

`compute_narrative()` is the orchestrator that takes `state` and optional `NarrativeContext` and produces a `NarrativeResult` dict with:

```python
return {
    "phase": phase,
    "headline": headline,
    "dimensions": dimensions,
    "actions": actions,              # Prioritized list of what to work on
    "strategy": strategy,            # Fixer leverage, lanes, parallelization hint
    "tools": tools,                  # Available fixers, move tool, plan command, badge
    "debt": debt,
    "milestone": milestone,
    "primary_action": primary_action,  # Top recommended action for user
    "why_now": why_now,              # Justification text
    "verification_step": verification_step,  # Always "desloppify scan"
    "risk_flags": risk_flags,        # Suppression/wontfix debt warnings
    "strict_target": strict_target,  # Current vs target strict score
    "reminders": reminders,          # Contextual coaching nudges
    "reminder_history": updated_reminder_history,
}
```

### 2. **Action Engine: How Actions Are Computed**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/action_engine.py`

The `compute_actions()` function (lines 259–270) builds a prioritized action list by:

1. **`_append_auto_fix_actions()`** (lines 91–144)
   - For each detector with `action_type == "auto_fix"`:
   - If fixers are available AND applicable findings exist:
     - Creates **auto_fix action** with command: `desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run`
   - Otherwise creates **manual_fix action** with command: `desloppify show {detector} --status open`
   - Example: "5 unused findings — run `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run` to fix all at once"

2. **`_append_reorganize_actions()`** (lines 147–171)
   - For detectors with `action_type == "reorganize"` (orphaned, flat_dirs, naming, single_use, coupling, cycles)
   - Command: `desloppify show {detector} --status open`
   - Guidance text from detector metadata (e.g., "delete dead files or relocate with `desloppify move`")

3. **`_append_refactor_actions()`** (lines 210–222)
   - For detectors with `action_type in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}` (structural, props, react, dupes, patterns, dict_keys, test_coverage, signature, global_mutable_config, private_imports, layer_violation, etc.)
   - Special handling for **review findings**:
     - Command: `desloppify show review --status open`
     - Description: "{count} review finding(s) need investigation"
   - Special handling for **subjective_review**:
     - Command: `desloppify review --prepare`
     - Description: "{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"

4. **`_append_debt_action()`** (lines 225–238)
   - If wontfix gap > 2.0 points, appends debt_review action
   - Command: `desloppify show --status wontfix`
   - Description: "{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions"

5. **`_assign_priorities()`** (lines 241–256)
   - Sorts by type priority: `issue_queue (0) → auto_fix (1) → reorganize (2) → refactor (3) → manual_fix (4) → debt_review (5)`
   - Then by impact (highest first)
   - Assigns sequential `priority` field (1, 2, 3, ...)

**Key Detail:** Each action has:
- `priority`: Sequential number (1-N)
- `type`: One of "auto_fix", "reorganize", "refactor", "manual_fix", "issue_queue", "debt_review"
- `detector`: Which detector this applies to
- `count`: Number of open findings
- `description`: User-facing narrative
- `command`: Exact CLI command to run
- `impact`: Estimated score improvement (1-decimal float)
- `dimension`: The dimension name affected (e.g., "Code quality")
- `lane`: (set later by strategy) which parallelizable work lane

### 3. **Strategy Engine: Execution Lanes & Parallelization**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/strategy_engine.py`

`compute_strategy()` (lines 299–337) takes findings, by_detector counts, actions, phase, lang and returns:

```python
{
    "fixer_leverage": {
        "auto_fixable_count": int,
        "total_count": int,
        "coverage": float (0.0–1.0),
        "impact_ratio": float (0.0–1.0),
        "recommendation": "strong" | "moderate" | "none"
    },
    "lanes": {
        "cleanup": { "actions": [1, 3, 5], "file_count": 12, "total_impact": 2.3, "automation": "full", "run_first": False },
        "restructure": { "actions": [2], "file_count": 8, "total_impact": 1.5, "automation": "manual", "run_first": False },
        "refactor": { "actions": [4, 6], "file_count": 15, "total_impact": 0.8, "automation": "manual", "run_first": False },
        ...
    },
    "can_parallelize": True,
    "hint": "Run fixers first — they cover 40% of findings. Then 2 independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. Rescan after each phase to verify. Review: 5 finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
}
```

**Lane Types:**
- **cleanup**: Auto-fix actions (full automation)
- **restructure**: Reorganize actions (move-based)
- **refactor**: Manual refactor tasks (groupable by file overlap via union-find)
- **test_coverage**: Separate lane for test coverage work
- **debt_review**: Wontfix debt actions

**`_group_by_file_overlap()`** (lines 92–125): Uses union-find to group refactor actions that affect overlapping file sets, creating independent lanes like `refactor_0`, `refactor_1`, etc.

**`compute_fixer_leverage()`** (lines 31–68): Estimates how many findings are auto-fixable:
- If `coverage > 0.4` or `impact_ratio > 0.3` → "strong" recommendation
- If `phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression")` and `coverage > 0.15` → "strong"
- If `coverage > 0.1` → "moderate"
- Otherwise → "none"

### 4. **Detector Registry: Individual Finding Metadata**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/registry.py`

All 32+ detectors are defined with:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DetectorMeta:
    name: str                      # e.g., "unused"
    display: str                   # Human-readable name
    dimension: str                 # Scoring dimension (e.g., "Code quality")
    action_type: str               # "auto_fix" | "reorganize" | "refactor" | "manual_fix"
    guidance: str                  # Narrative coaching text
    fixers: tuple[str, ...]        # Available fixers (e.g., ("unused-imports", "unused-vars"))
    tool: str = ""                 # "move" for structural reorganization tools
    structural: bool = False       # Merges under "structural" for display
    needs_judgment: bool = False   # Findings require LLM judgment
```

**Example Entries:**

| Detector | Dimension | Action Type | Guidance | Fixers | Tool |
|----------|-----------|------------|----------|--------|------|
| unused | Code quality | auto_fix | remove unused imports and variables | (unused-imports, unused-vars, unused-params) | |
| logs | Code quality | auto_fix | remove debug logs | (debug-logs,) | |
| orphaned | Code quality | reorganize | delete dead files or relocate with `desloppify move` | () | move |
| structural (merged: large, complexity, gods, concerns) | File health | refactor | decompose large files | () | |
| react | Code quality | refactor | refactor React antipatterns | () | |
| review | Test health | refactor | address design quality findings from AI code review | () | |
| subjective_review | Test health | manual_fix | run `desloppify review --prepare` to evaluate files | () | |

**`STRUCTURAL_MERGE` set** (constants.py line 30): `{"large", "complexity", "gods", "concerns"}` — these detectors are displayed/counted under "structural" in narrative outputs.

```python
{

    },

}
```

### 6. **Individual Finding Guidance in Next Command**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` and `/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`

When `desloppify next` renders a single finding card (lines 147–399 of render.py):

1. **Tier & Confidence**:
   ```
   (Tier 2, high confidence)
   ```

2. **Summary & Detail**:
   ```
   Unused variable 'oldCount' at line 42 of src/components/Counter.tsx
   ```

3. **Effort Type Label**:
   - For `detector == "review"`: `Type: Design review (requires judgment)`
   - For auto-fixable: `Type: Auto-fixable`

4. **Primary Command** (computed in `primary_command_for_finding()`, helpers.py line 151–168):
   ```python
   if detector is "auto_fix" type and has fixers:
       return f"desloppify fix {first_available_fixer} --dry-run"
   elif detector == "subjective_review":
       if is_holistic: return "desloppify review --prepare"
       else: return "desloppify show subjective"
   else:
       return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{id}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."'
   ```

5. **Dimension & Score Impact**:
   ```
   Dimension: Code quality — 75.0% (strict: 72.5%) (8 of 12 checks failing)
   Impact: fixing this is worth ~+0.3 pts on overall score
   ```

6. **Auto-fixable Batch Info** (for auto-fix commands):
   ```
   Auto-fixable: 8 similar findings. Run `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run` to fix all at once.
   ```

7. **Resolution Hints** (render.py lines 402–436):
   ```
   Fix with:
       desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run
   Or resolve individually:
       desloppify resolve fixed "path/file.ts::line:42" --note "<what>" --attest "..."
       desloppify resolve wontfix "..." --note "<why>" --attest "..."
   ```

### 7. **Cluster Support in Next Command**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

Clusters are plan metadata that group related findings:

1. **Auto-Collapsed Clusters** (lines 172–247):
   - When plan has `clusters` data with `auto: True`, multiple findings are collapsed into a single "cluster meta-item"
   - Shown only if member count ≥ 2 (singletons remain individual)

2. **Cluster Item Rendering** (render.py lines 100–144):
   ```
   (Auto-fixable batch, 12 findings)
   ──────────────────────────────────
   12 unused imports across 3 files
   
   Files:
     src/utils.ts (5)
     src/api.ts (4)
     src/types.ts (3)
   
   Sample:
     - src/utils.ts::import from 'lodash'
     - src/api.ts::import from 'axios'
     - src/types.ts::import from '@types/node'
     ... and 9 more
   
   Action: desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run
   
   Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."
   Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10
   Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}
   ```

3. **Plan Metadata on Individual Items** (work_queue.py lines 84–99):
   - Each item may have:
     - `plan_description`: User override description
     - `plan_note`: Sticky note from plan
     - `plan_cluster`: Reference to parent cluster
     - `plan_skipped`: Boolean if skipped
     - `queue_position`: Position in the living plan order

### 8. **Integration: How Narrative Flows to Next Command**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

Flow (lines 61–195):

1. **Build work queue** with plan (if any):
   ```python
   queue = build_work_queue(
       state,
       options=QueueBuildOptions(
           tier=tier,
           plan=plan_data,       # Living plan
           cluster=effective_cluster,
           ...
       ),
   )
   ```

2. **Compute narrative**:
   ```python
   narrative = compute_narrative(
       state,
       context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   )
   ```

3. **Build output payload**:
   ```python
   payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
       queue, items,
       command="next",
       narrative=narrative,  # Full narrative dict
       plan=plan_data,       # Living plan metadata
   )
   ```

4. **Write query.json** with full narrative structure
5. **Render terminal** with:
   - Queue header (tier navigator)
   - Items (with plan overrides, cluster info, dimension scores, impact estimates)
   - Resolution hints (commands to fix or resolve)
   - Followup nudges (subjective dimensions, integrity warnings, review findings)

### 9. **Key Constants & Shared State**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/_constants.py`

```python
DETECTOR_TOOLS = _detector_tools()  # Built from registry.DETECTORS

STRUCTURAL_MERGE = {"large", "complexity", "gods", "concerns"}
# When counting open by detector, these merge under "structural"

_DETECTOR_CASCADE = {
    "logs": ["unused"],      # Fixing logs may auto-resolve unused
    "smells": ["unused"],
}

_REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD = 3  # Suppress reminders after 3 shows
```

**Detector tools are refreshed** when detectors are registered at runtime.

### 10. **Summary: What the User Sees**

When a user runs **`desloppify next`** or views query.json:

**Per-finding card:**
- Tier, confidence, detector, file, ID
- Summary + detail (lines, category, suggestion, code snippet)
- Dimension + dimension score + strict score
- Score impact estimate
- Type label (auto-fixable, review, etc.)
- **Primary action command** (auto-fix command OR resolve template)
- Plan overrides (description, note, cluster membership, skip status)
- Explanation (if --explain flag)

**At plan level:**
- All actions from narrative (prioritized, impact-rated, lane-assigned)
- Strategy hint (fixer coverage, parallelization advice, review count)
- Tools available (fixers with commands, move tool, plan command)

**Narrative context:**
- Phase (first_scan, regression, stagnation, maintenance)
- Headline (tailored progress message)
- Dimensions (per-dimension scores and risk)
- Primary action (top-priority command to run now)
- Why now (justification)
- Risk flags (high suppression, wontfix debt warnings)
- Reminders (contextual coaching nudges, decay-suppressed after 3 shows)

**Plan integration** (if active):
- Living plan reorders findings per user's queue_order
- Clusters collapse auto-generated groups
- Focused cluster mode (–cluster flag) filters to one cluster's members
- Cluster drilling (show individual members or resolve as batch)
- Skip annotations (items may be marked as temporarily/permanently skipped)

### 11. **Narrative Does NOT Currently Know About Clusters Deeply**

The narrative/action system computes **generic actions per detector** (e.g., "5 unused findings"). It does **not**:
- See individual cluster definitions from the plan
- Recommend cluster-specific strategies
- Adjust action_type based on cluster membership
- Provide cluster-aware lane assignments

**Clusters are applied post-narrative** in the work queue builder (`_apply_plan_order()` and `_collapse_clusters()`), which:
1. Uses plan metadata to annotate items
2. Collapses finding members into cluster meta-items
3. Preserves queue order from the plan

This is by design — clusters are a **user-driven organization layer**, not a narrative AI recommendation.
