```
   325→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```
```

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→
```

```
1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    add_to_cluster,
    13→    create_cluster,
    14→    delete_cluster,
    15→    load_plan,
    16→    move_cluster,
    17→    remove_from_cluster,
    18→    save_plan,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    plan = load_plan()
    26→    try:
    27→        create_cluster(plan, name, description)
    28→    except ValueError as ex:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    30→        return
    31→    save_plan(plan)
    32→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    37→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    41→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    42→
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    45→    if not finding_ids:
    46→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    47→        return
    48→    try:
    49→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    50→    except ValueError as ex:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    52→        return
    53→    save_plan(plan)
    54→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    59→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    63→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    64→
    65→    plan = load_plan()
    66→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    67→    if not finding_ids:
    68→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→    try:
    71→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    72→    except ValueError as ex:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    74→        return
    75→    save_plan(plan)
    76→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    80→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    81→    plan = load_plan()
    82→    try:
    83→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    84→    except ValueError as ex:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    86→        return
    87→    save_plan(plan)
    88→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    94→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    95→
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→
    98→    offset: int | None = None
    99→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   100→        try:
   101→            offset = int(target)
   102→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   103→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   104→            return
   105→        target = None
   106→
   107→    try:
   108→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   109→    except ValueError as ex:
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   111→        return
   112→    save_plan(plan)
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→    if not clusters:
   121→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   122→        return
   123→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   124→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   127→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   130→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   131→
   132→
   133→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   134→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   135→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   136→    dispatch = {
   137→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   138→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   139→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   140→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   141→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   142→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   143→    }
   144→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   145→    if handler is None:
   146→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   147→        return
   148→    handler(args)
   149→
   150→
   151→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   152→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
   248→) -> Cluster:
   249→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   250→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   251→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   252→        raise ValueError(
   253→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   254→        )
   255→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   256→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   257→    now = utc_now()
   258→    cluster: Cluster = {
   259→        "name": name,
   260→        "description": description,
   261→        "finding_ids": [],
   262→        "created_at": now,
   263→        "updated_at": now,
   264→        "auto": False,
   265→        "cluster_key": "",
   266→        "action": None,
   267→        "user_modified": False,
   268→    }
   269→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   270→    return cluster
   271→
   272→
   273→def add_to_cluster(
   274→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   275→) -> int:
   276→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   279→    if cluster is None:
   280→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   281→
   282→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   283→    count = 0
   284→    now = utc_now()
   285→    for fid in finding_ids:
   286→        if fid not in member_ids:
   287→            member_ids.append(fid)
   288→            count += 1
   289→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   290→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   291→        if fid not in overrides:
   292→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   293→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   294→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   295→
   296→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   297→    return count
   298→
   299→
   300→def remove_from_cluster(
   301→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   302→) -> int:
   303→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   304→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   305→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   306→    if cluster is None:
   307→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   308→
   309→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   310→    now = utc_now()
   311→    count = 0
   312→    for fid in finding_ids:
   313→        if fid in member_ids:
   314→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   315→            count += 1
   316→        # Clear cluster from override
   317→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   318→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   319→            override["cluster"] = None
   320→            override["updated_at"] = now
   321→
   322→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   323→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   324→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   325→
   326→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   327→    return count
   328→
   329→
   330→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   331→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   332→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   333→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   334→    if cluster is None:
   335→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   336→
   337→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   338→    now = utc_now()
   339→    for fid in orphaned:
   340→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   341→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   342→            override["cluster"] = None
   343→            override["updated_at"] = now
   344→
   345→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   346→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   347→
   348→    return orphaned
   349→
   350→
   351→def move_cluster(
   352→    plan: PlanModel,
   353→    cluster_name: str,
   354→    position: str,
   355→    target: str | None = None,
   356→    offset: int | None = None,
   357→) -> int:
   358→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   359→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   360→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   361→    if cluster is None:
   362→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   363→
   364→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   365→    if not member_ids:
   366→        return 0
   367→
   368→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   369→
   370→
   371→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   372→# Focus
   373→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   374→
   375→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   376→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   377→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   378→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   379→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   380→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   381→
   382→
   383→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   384→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   385→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   386→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   387→
   388→
   389→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   390→# Reset
   391→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   392→
   393→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   394→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   395→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   396→    plan.clear()
   397→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   398→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   399→        plan[k] = v
   400→    plan["created"] = created
   401→
   402→
   403→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   404→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   405→
   406→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   407→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   408→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   409→    """
   410→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   411→    found = 0
   412→
   413→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   414→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   415→    for fid in finding_ids:
   416→        was_present = False
   417→        if fid in order:
   418→            order.remove(fid)
   419→            was_present = True
   420→        if fid in skipped:
   421→            skipped.pop(fid)
   422→            was_present = True
   423→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   424→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   425→            if fid in ids:
   426→                ids.remove(fid)
   427→                was_present = True
   428→        if was_present:
   429→            found += 1
   430→
   431→    return found
   432→
   433→
   434→__all__ = [
   435→    "add_to_cluster",
   436→    "annotate_finding",
   437→    "clear_focus",
   438→    "create_cluster",
   439→    "delete_cluster",
   440→    "describe_finding",
   441→    "move_cluster",
   442→    "move_items",
   443→    "purge_ids",
   444→    "remove_from_cluster",
   445→    "reset_plan",
   446→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   447→    "set_focus",
   448→    "skip_items",
   449→    "unskip_items",
   450→]
   451→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
```

```
    41→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    42→    p_scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff")
    43→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    44→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
    45→    p_scan.add_argument(
    46→        "--reset-subjective",
    47→        action="store_true",
    48→        help="Reset subjective measures to 0 before running scan",
    49→    )
    50→    p_scan.add_argument(
    51→        "--skip-slow", action="store_true", help="Skip slow detectors (dupes)"
    52→    )
    53→    p_scan.add_argument(
    54→        "--profile",
    55→        choices=["objective", "full", "ci"],
    56→        default=None,
    57→        help="Scan profile: objective, full, or ci",
    58→    )
    59→    p_scan.add_argument(
    60→        "--force-resolve",
    61→        action="store_true",
    62→        help="Bypass suspect-detector protection (use when a detector legitimately went to 0)",
    63→    )
    64→    p_scan.add_argument(
    65→        "--no-badge",
    66→        action="store_true",
    67→        help="Skip scorecard image generation (also: DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE=true)",
    68→    )
    69→    p_scan.add_argument(
    70→        "--badge-path",
    71→        type=str,
    72→        default=None,
    73→        metavar="PATH",
    74→        help="Output path for scorecard image (default: scorecard.png)",
    75→    )
    76→    p_scan.add_argument(
    77→        "--lang-opt",
    78→        action="append",
    79→        default=None,
    80→        metavar="KEY=VALUE",
    81→        help="Language runtime option override (repeatable, e.g. --lang-opt roslyn_cmd='dotnet run ...')",
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def _add_status_parser(sub) -> None:
    86→    p_status = sub.add_parser("status", help="Score dashboard with per-tier progress")
    87→    p_status.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
    88→    p_status.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    89→
    90→
    91→def _add_tree_parser(sub) -> None:
    92→    p_tree = sub.add_parser("tree", help="Annotated codebase tree (text)")
    93→    p_tree.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    94→    p_tree.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
    95→    p_tree.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=2, help="Max depth (default: 2)")
    96→    p_tree.add_argument(
    97→        "--focus",
    98→        type=str,
    99→        default=None,
   100→        help="Zoom into subdirectory (e.g. shared/components/MediaLightbox)",
   101→    )
   102→    p_tree.add_argument(
   103→        "--min-loc", type=int, default=0, help="Hide items below this LOC"
   104→    )
   105→    p_tree.add_argument(
   106→        "--sort", choices=["loc", "findings", "coupling"], default="loc"
   107→    )
   108→    p_tree.add_argument(
   109→        "--detail", action="store_true", help="Show finding summaries per file"
   110→    )

   113→def _add_show_parser(sub) -> None:
   114→    p_show = sub.add_parser(
   115→        "show", help="Dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or ID"
   116→    )
   117→    p_show.add_argument(
   118→        "pattern",
   119→        nargs="?",

   121→        help="File path, directory, detector name, finding ID, or glob",

   127→        default="open",

   129→    p_show.add_argument(
   130→        "--top", type=int, default=20, help="Max files to show (default: 20)"
```

```
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)
```

## COMPREHENSIVE FINDINGS REPORT

### 1. **Plan Cluster Subcommands** (from `parser_groups_admin.py` lines 535-568)

**Registered cluster subcommands:**
- `plan cluster create <name> [--description "..."]` — Create a new cluster
- `plan cluster add <cluster> <patterns...>` — Add findings to a cluster
- `plan cluster remove <cluster> <patterns...>` — Remove findings from a cluster
- `plan cluster delete <name>` — Delete a cluster
- `plan cluster move <cluster> <position> [target]` — Move cluster as a contiguous block
- `plan cluster list` — List all clusters (default if no action specified)

**There is NO `plan cluster show <name>` subcommand.** The only way to view cluster contents is via `plan cluster list` which shows summary info or `plan show` which shows plan metadata.

---

### 2. **Cluster Handler Implementation** (from `cluster_handlers.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`

**Handlers:**
- `_cmd_cluster_create()` — Accepts `cluster_name` and `description` (optional). Calls `create_cluster(plan, name, description)`. **Does NOT accept an action parameter.**
- `_cmd_cluster_add()` — Resolves patterns to finding_ids, calls `add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)`
- `_cmd_cluster_remove()` — Removes findings from cluster
- `_cmd_cluster_delete()` — Deletes cluster, returns orphaned IDs
- `_cmd_cluster_move()` — Moves cluster members as a block
- `_cmd_cluster_list()` — Lists all clusters with name, member count, description, focused marker, and auto-marker
- `cmd_cluster_dispatch()` — Routes to handlers; defaults to list if no action

---

### 3. **Cluster Creation Flow** (from `operations.py` lines 246-270)

```python
def create_cluster(
    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
) -> Cluster:
    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    if name.startswith("auto/"):
        raise ValueError(...)
    if name in plan["clusters"]:
        raise ValueError(...)
    now = utc_now()
    cluster: Cluster = {
        "name": name,
        "description": description,
        "finding_ids": [],
        "created_at": now,
        "updated_at": now,
        "auto": False,
        "cluster_key": "",
        "action": None,           # <-- KEY: action field is ALWAYS None for manual clusters
        "user_modified": False,
    }
    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
    return cluster
```

**CRITICAL FINDING:** The `action` field in the Cluster schema is initialized to `None` and there is **NO parameter to accept an action during creation**. The field exists but is not populated by `create_cluster()`.

---

### 4. **Detector Registry: Complete List with Guidance** (from `registry.py` lines 59-321)

All detectors are `DetectorMeta` dataclass instances with fields: `name`, `display`, `dimension`, `action_type`, `guidance`, `fixers`, `tool`, `structural`, `needs_judgment`.

**DETECTORS WITH `needs_judgment=True`:**

| Name | Display | Dimension | Action Type | Guidance | Fixers | Tool | Judgment |
|------|---------|-----------|-------------|----------|--------|------|----------|
| `smells` | smells | Code quality | auto_fix | "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" | (dead-useeffect, empty-if-chain) | | YES |
| `orphaned` | orphaned | Code quality | reorganize | "delete dead files or relocate with `desloppify move`" | | move | YES |
| `uncalled_functions` | uncalled functions | Code quality | refactor | "remove dead functions or document why they're retained" | | | YES |
| `flat_dirs` | flat dirs | Code quality | reorganize | "create subdirectories and use `desloppify move`" | | move | YES |
| `naming` | naming | Code quality | reorganize | "rename files with `desloppify move` to fix conventions" | | move | YES |
| `single_use` | single_use | Code quality | reorganize | "inline or relocate with `desloppify move`" | | move | YES |
| `coupling` | coupling | Code quality | reorganize | "fix boundary violations with `desloppify move`" | | move | YES |
| `cycles` | cycles | Security | reorganize | "break cycles by extracting shared code or using `desloppify move`" | | move | YES |
| `facade` | facade | Code quality | reorganize | "flatten re-export facades or consolidate barrel files" | | move | YES |
| `structural` | structural | File health | refactor | "decompose large files — extract logic into focused modules" | | | YES |
| `props` | props | Code quality | refactor | "split bloated components, extract sub-components" | | | YES |
| `react` | react | Code quality | refactor | "refactor React antipatterns (state sync, provider nesting, hook bloat)" | | | YES |
| `dupes` | dupes | Duplication | refactor | "extract shared utility or consolidate duplicates" | | | YES |
| `patterns` | patterns | Code quality | refactor | "align to single pattern across the codebase" | | | YES |
| `dict_keys` | dict keys | Code quality | refactor | "fix dict key mismatches — dead writes are likely dead code, schema drift suggests a typo or missed rename" | | | YES |
| `signature` | signature | Code quality | refactor | "consolidate inconsistent function signatures" | | | YES |
| `global_mutable_config` | global mutable config | Code quality | manual_fix | "refactor module-level mutable state — use explicit init functions or dependency injection" | | | YES |
| `private_imports` | private imports | Code quality | manual_fix | "stop importing private symbols across module boundaries" | | | YES |
| `layer_violation` | layer violation | Code quality | manual_fix | "fix architectural layer violations — move shared code to the correct layer" | | | YES |
| `responsibility_cohesion` | responsibility cohesion | Code quality | refactor | "split modules with too many responsibilities — extract focused sub-modules" | | | YES |
| `boilerplate_duplication` | boilerplate duplication | Duplication | refactor | "extract shared boilerplate into reusable helpers or base classes" | | | YES |

**ALL OTHER DETECTORS (no judgment required):**

| Name | Guidance | Action Type |
|------|----------|------------|
| `unused` | "remove unused imports and variables" | auto_fix |
| `logs` | "remove debug logs" | auto_fix |
| `exports` | "run `knip --fix` to remove dead exports" | manual_fix |
| `deprecated` | "remove deprecated symbols or migrate callers" | manual_fix |
| `stale_exclude` | "remove stale exclusion or verify it's still needed" | manual_fix |
| `security` | "review and fix security findings — prioritize by severity" | manual_fix |
| `test_coverage` | "add tests for untested production modules — prioritize by import count" | refactor |
| `stale_wontfix` | "re-evaluate old wontfix decisions — fix, document, or escalate" | manual_fix |
| `concerns` | "address design concerns confirmed by subjective evaluation" | refactor |
| `review` | "address design quality findings from AI code review" | refactor |
| `subjective_review` | "run `desloppify review --prepare` to evaluate files against quality dimensions" | manual_fix |

---

### 5. **Primary Command Determination** (from `_work_queue/helpers.py` lines 151-168)

```python
def primary_command_for_finding(
    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
) -> str:
    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
        available_fixers = [
            fixer
            for fixer in meta.fixers
            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
        ]
        if available_fixers:
            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
    if detector == "subjective_review":
        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
            return "desloppify review --prepare"
        return "desloppify show subjective"
    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
```

**Decision Logic:**
1. If detector has `action_type == "auto_fix"` AND has fixers, return first fixer (if supported): `desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run`
2. If detector is `subjective_review`:
   - If holistic finding → `desloppify review --prepare`
   - Else → `desloppify show subjective`
3. Default (all other cases) → `desloppify resolve fixed "<id>" --note "<...>" --attest "<...>"`

---

### 6. **Key Architectural Notes**

- **No Cluster Action Parameter:** The `create_cluster()` function signature is:
  ```python
  def create_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None) -> Cluster
  ```
  It does NOT accept an `action` parameter. The cluster's `action` field is always initialized to `None`.

- **Cluster Schema** contains these fields:
  - `name`, `description`, `finding_ids`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
  - `auto` (boolean, True for auto-generated clusters)
  - `cluster_key` (string, empty for manual clusters)
  - `action` (always None for manual clusters; likely for future use or auto-clusters)
  - `user_modified` (tracks if user manually modified an auto-cluster)

- **No "show cluster" command exists.** Only `plan cluster list` and `plan show` can display cluster info.

---

## SUMMARY

**For viewing cluster contents:** Use `desloppify plan cluster list` (shows all clusters with member counts) or `desloppify plan show` (shows plan metadata including clusters).

**For action guidance:** Each detector's guidance is stored in `DETECTORS` registry in `core/registry.py`. The `primary_command_for_finding()` function automatically selects the appropriate action command based on the detector's `action_type`.

**For creating clusters with actions:** Currently not possible through the CLI. The `action` field in Cluster exists but is not settable—it's always `None` for manual clusters. This may be a future-planned feature.

```
   322→
   323→_DISPLAY_ORDER = list(DISPLAY_ORDER)
   324→
   325→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```

```
   190→def _build_string_line_set(lines: list[str]) -> set[int]:
   191→    """Build a set of 0-indexed line numbers that are inside multi-line strings.
   192→
   193→    Tracks triple-quote state across lines so regex-based checks can skip
   194→    lines that are inside multi-line string literals.
   195→    """
   196→    in_multiline: str | None = None  # '"""' or "'''" or None
   197→    string_lines: set[int] = set()
   198→
   199→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   200→        if in_multiline is not None:
   201→            string_lines.add(i)
   202→            # Check if this line closes the multi-line string
   203→            if in_multiline in line:
   204→                # Find the closing triple-quote (skip escaped ones)
   205→                pos = 0
   206→                while pos < len(line):
   207→                    idx = line.find(in_multiline, pos)
   208→                    if idx == -1:
   209→                        break
   210→                    # Check it's not escaped
   211→                    backslashes = 0
   212→                    j = idx - 1
   213→                    while j >= 0 and line[j] == "\\":
   214→                        backslashes += 1
   215→                        j -= 1
   216→                    if backslashes % 2 == 0:
   217→                        in_multiline = None
   218→                        break
   219→                    pos = idx + 3
   220→            continue
   221→
   222→        # Check if this line opens a multi-line string
   223→        pos = 0
   224→        while pos < len(line):
   225→            ch = line[pos]
   226→            if ch == "#":
   227→                break  # Rest is comment
   228→            # Skip string prefixes
   229→            if ch in ("r", "b", "f", "u", "R", "B", "F", "U") and pos + 1 < len(line):
   230→                next_ch = line[pos + 1]
   231→                if next_ch in ('"', "'"):
   232→                    pos += 1
   233→                    ch = next_ch
   234→                elif (
   235→                    next_ch in ("r", "b", "f", "R", "B", "F")
   236→                    and pos + 2 < len(line)
   237→                    and line[pos + 2] in ('"', "'")
   238→                ):
   239→                    pos += 2
   240→                    ch = line[pos]
   241→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
   242→                triple = line[pos : pos + 3]
   243→                if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
   244→                    # Check if it closes on the same line
   245→                    close_idx = line.find(triple, pos + 3)
   246→                    if close_idx == -1:
   247→                        # Opens a multi-line string
   248→                        in_multiline = triple
   249→                        break
   250→                    else:
   251→                        pos = close_idx + 3
   252→                        continue
   253→                else:
   254→                    # Single-line string — skip to closing quote
   255→                    end = line.find(ch, pos + 1)
   256→                    while end != -1 and end > 0 and line[end - 1] == "\\":
   257→                        end = line.find(ch, end + 1)
   258→                    pos = (end + 1) if end != -1 else len(line)
   259→                    continue
   260→            pos += 1
   261→
   262→    return string_lines
   263→
   264→
   265→def _match_is_in_string(line: str, match_start: int) -> bool:
   266→    """Check if a regex match position falls inside a string literal or comment on a single line."""
   267→    i, in_string = 0, None
   268→    while i < len(line):
   269→        if i == match_start:
   270→            return in_string is not None
   271→        ch = line[i]
   272→        if in_string is None:
   273→            if ch == "#":
   274→                return True  # In a comment, not real code
   275→            triple = line[i : i + 3]
   276→            if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
   277→                in_string = triple
   278→                i += 3
   279→                continue
   280→            if (
   281→                ch in ("r", "b", "f")
   282→                and i + 1 < len(line)
   283→                and line[i + 1] in ('"', "'")
   284→            ):
   285→                i += 1
   286→                ch = line[i]
   287→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
   288→                in_string = ch
   289→                i += 1
   290→                continue
   291→        else:
   292→            if ch == "\\" and i + 1 < len(line):
   293→                i += 2
   294→                continue
   295→            if in_string in ('"""', "'''"):
   296→                if line[i : i + 3] == in_string:
   297→                    in_string = None
   298→                    i += 3
   299→                    continue
   300→            elif ch == in_string:
   301→                in_string = None
   302→                i += 1
   303→                continue
   304→        i += 1
   305→    return in_string is not None
   306→
   307→
   308→def detect_smells(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   309→    """Detect Python code smell patterns. Returns (entries, total_files_checked)."""
   310→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {s["id"]: [] for s in SMELL_CHECKS}
   311→    files = find_py_files(path)
   312→    # Collect module-level constants for cross-file duplicate detection
   313→    constants_by_key: dict[tuple[str, str], list[tuple[str, int]]] = {}
   314→
   315→    for filepath in files:
   316→        try:
   317→            p = (
   318→                Path(filepath)
   319→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   320→                else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   321→            )
   322→            content = p.read_text()
   323→            lines = content.splitlines()
   324→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   325→            log_best_effort_failure(
   326→                logger, f"read Python file for smell scan {filepath}", exc
   327→            )
   328→            continue
   329→
   330→        if _is_test_path(filepath):
   331→            continue
   332→
   333→        # Build set of lines inside multi-line strings to avoid false positives
   334→        multiline_string_lines = _build_string_line_set(lines)
   335→
   336→        for check in SMELL_CHECKS:
   337→            if check["pattern"] is None:
   338→                continue
   339→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   340→                # Skip lines inside multi-line strings
   341→                if i in multiline_string_lines:
   342→                    continue
   343→                m = re.search(check["pattern"], line)
   344→                if m and not _match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   345→                    # Skip URLs assigned to module-level constants (UPPER_CASE = "https://...")
   346→                    if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   347→                        r"^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=", line.strip()
   348→                    ):
   349→                        continue
   350→                    smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   351→                        {
   352→                            "file": filepath,
   353→                            "line": i + 1,
   354→                            "content": line.strip()[:100],
   355→                        }
   356→                    )
   357→
   358→        _detect_empty_except(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   359→        _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   360→        detect_ast_smells(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   361→        detect_star_import_no_all(filepath, content, path, smell_counts)
   362→        detect_vestigial_parameter(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   363→        collect_module_constants(filepath, content, constants_by_key)
   364→
   365→    # Cross-file: detect duplicate constants
   366→    detect_duplicate_constants(constants_by_key, smell_counts)
   367→
   368→    severity_order = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
   369→    entries = []
   370→    for check in SMELL_CHECKS:
   371→        matches = smell_counts[check["id"]]
   372→        if matches:
   373→            entries.append(
   374→                {
   375→                    "id": check["id"],
   376→                    "label": check["label"],
   377→                    "severity": check["severity"],
   378→                    "count": len(matches),
   379→                    "files": len(set(m["file"] for m in matches)),
   380→                    "matches": matches[:50],
   381→                }
   382→            )
   383→    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (severity_order.get(e["severity"], 9), -e["count"]))
   384→    return entries, len(files)
   385→
   386→
   387→def _walk_except_blocks(lines: list[str]):
   388→    """Yield (line_index, except_line_stripped, body_lines) for each except block."""
   389→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   390→        stripped = line.strip()
   391→        if not re.match(r"except\s*(?:\w|\(|:)", stripped) and stripped != "except:":
   392→            continue
   393→        if not stripped.endswith(":"):
   394→            continue
   395→        indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())
   396→        j, body_lines = i + 1, []
   397→        while j < len(lines):
   398→            next_line = lines[j]
   399→            next_stripped = next_line.strip()
   400→            if next_stripped == "":
   401→                j += 1
   402→                continue
   403→            if len(next_line) - len(next_line.lstrip()) <= indent:
   404→                break
   405→            body_lines.append(next_stripped)
   406→            j += 1
   407→        yield i, stripped, body_lines
   408→
   409→
   410→def _is_broad_except(stripped: str) -> bool:
   411→    """Check if except clause catches broadly (bare, Exception, BaseException)."""
   412→    if stripped == "except:":
   413→        return True
   414→    m = re.match(r"except\s+(\w+)", stripped)
   415→    return bool(m and m.group(1) in ("Exception", "BaseException"))
   416→
   417→
   418→def _detect_empty_except(
   419→    filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list]
   420→):
   421→    """Find broad except blocks that just pass or have empty body."""
   422→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   423→        if (not body_lines or body_lines == ["pass"]) and _is_broad_except(stripped):
   424→            smell_counts["empty_except"].append(
   425→                {
   426→                    "file": filepath,
   427→                    "line": i + 1,
   428→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   429→                }
   430→            )
   431→
   432→
   433→def _detect_swallowed_errors(
   434→    filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list]
   435→):
   436→    """Find except blocks that only print/log the error."""
   437→    _LOG_RE = r"(?:print|logging\.\w+|logger\.\w+|log\.\w+)\s*\("
   438→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   439→        if body_lines and all(re.match(_LOG_RE, s) for s in body_lines):
   440→            smell_counts["swallowed_error"].append(
   441→                {
   442→                    "file": filepath,
   443→                    "line": i + 1,
   444→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   445→                }
   446→            )
   447→
```
