```
   220→
   221→def _build_ts_line_state(lines: list[str]) -> dict[int, str]:
   222→    """Build a map of line numbers that are inside block comments or template literals.
   223→
   224→    Returns {0-indexed line: reason} where reason is "block_comment" or "template_literal".
   225→    Lines not in the map are normal code lines suitable for regex checks.
   226→
   227→    Tracks:
   228→    - Block comment state (opened by /*, closed by */)
   229→    - Template literal state (opened by backtick, closed by backtick,
   230→      with ${} nesting awareness)
   231→    """
   232→    state: dict[int, str] = {}
   233→    in_block_comment = False
   234→    in_template = False
   235→    template_brace_depth = 0  # tracks ${} nesting inside template literals
   236→
   237→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   238→        if in_block_comment:
   239→            state[i] = "block_comment"
   240→            if "*/" in line:
   241→                in_block_comment = False
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        if in_template:
   245→            state[i] = "template_literal"
   246→            # Scan for closing backtick or ${} nesting
   247→            j = 0
   248→            while j < len(line):
   249→                ch = line[j]
   250→                if ch == "\\" and j + 1 < len(line):
   251→                    j += 2
   252→                    continue
   253→                if ch == "$" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == "{":
   254→                    template_brace_depth += 1
   255→                    j += 2
   256→                    continue
   257→                if ch == "}" and template_brace_depth > 0:
   258→                    template_brace_depth -= 1
   259→                    j += 1
   260→                    continue
   261→                if ch == "`" and template_brace_depth == 0:
   262→                    in_template = False
   263→                    # Rest of line is normal code — don't mark it
   264→                    # but we already marked the line; that's fine for
   265→                    # line-level filtering
   266→                    break
   267→                j += 1
   268→            continue
   269→
   270→        # Normal code line — check for block comment or template literal start
   271→        j = 0
   272→        in_str = None
   273→        while j < len(line):
   274→            ch = line[j]
   275→
   276→            # Skip escape sequences
   277→            if in_str and ch == "\\" and j + 1 < len(line):
   278→                j += 2
   279→                continue
   280→
   281→            # String tracking
   282→            if in_str:
   283→                if ch == in_str:
   284→                    in_str = None
   285→                j += 1
   286→                continue
   287→
   288→            # Line comment — rest is not code
   289→            if ch == "/" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == "/":
   290→                break
   291→
   292→            # Block comment start
   293→            if ch == "/" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == "*":
   294→                # Check if it closes on same line
   295→                close = line.find("*/", j + 2)
   296→                if close != -1:
   297→                    j = close + 2
   298→                    continue
   299→                else:
   300→                    in_block_comment = True
   301→                    break
   302→
   303→            # Template literal start
   304→            if ch == "`":
   305→                # Scan for closing backtick on same line
   306→                k = j + 1
   307→                found_close = False
   308→                depth = 0
   309→                while k < len(line):
   310→                    c = line[k]
   311→                    if c == "\\" and k + 1 < len(line):
   312→                        k += 2
   313→                        continue
   314→                    if c == "$" and k + 1 < len(line) and line[k + 1] == "{":
   315→                        depth += 1
   316→                        k += 2
   317→                        continue
   318→                    if c == "}" and depth > 0:
   319→                        depth -= 1
   320→                        k += 1
   321→                        continue
   322→                    if c == "`" and depth == 0:
   323→                        found_close = True
   324→                        j = k + 1
   325→                        break
   326→                    k += 1
   327→                if found_close:
   328→                    continue
   329→                else:
   330→                    in_template = True
   331→                    template_brace_depth = depth
   332→                    break
   333→
   334→            if ch in ("'", '"'):
   335→                in_str = ch
   336→                j += 1
   337→                continue
   338→
   339→            j += 1
   340→
   341→    return state
   342→
   343→
   344→def _detect_empty_if_chains(filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> None:
   345→    """Find if/else chains where all branches are empty."""
   346→    index = 0
   347→    while index < len(lines):
   348→        stripped = lines[index].strip()
   349→        if not re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\(", stripped):
   350→            index += 1
   351→            continue
   352→
   353→        if re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", stripped):
   354→            chain_start = index
   355→            cursor = index + 1
   356→            while cursor < len(lines):
   357→                next_stripped = lines[cursor].strip()
   358→                if re.match(r"else\s+if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", next_stripped):
   359→                    cursor += 1
   360→                    continue
   361→                if re.match(r"(?:\}\s*)?else\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", next_stripped):
   362→                    cursor += 1
   363→                    continue
   364→                break
   365→            smell_counts["empty_if_chain"].append(
   366→                {"file": filepath, "line": chain_start + 1, "content": stripped[:100]}
   367→            )
   368→            index = cursor
   369→            continue
   370→
   371→        if re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", stripped):
   372→            chain_start = index
   373→            chain_all_empty = True
   374→            cursor = index
   375→            while cursor < len(lines):
   376→                current = lines[cursor].strip()
   377→                if cursor == chain_start:
   378→                    if not re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", current):
   379→                        chain_all_empty = False
   380→                        break
   381→                elif re.match(r"\}\s*else\s+if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", current):
   382→                    pass
   383→                elif re.match(r"\}\s*else\s*\{\s*$", current):
   384→                    pass
   385→                elif current == "}":
   386→                    tail = cursor + 1
   387→                    while tail < len(lines) and lines[tail].strip() == "":
   388→                        tail += 1
   389→                    if tail < len(lines) and re.match(r"else\s", lines[tail].strip()):
   390→                        cursor = tail
   391→                        continue
   392→                    cursor += 1
   393→                    break
   394→                elif current == "":
   395→                    cursor += 1
   396→                    continue
   397→                else:
   398→                    chain_all_empty = False
   399→                    break
   400→                cursor += 1
   401→            if chain_all_empty and cursor > chain_start + 1:
   402→                smell_counts["empty_if_chain"].append(
   403→                    {"file": filepath, "line": chain_start + 1, "content": lines[chain_start].strip()[:100]}
   404→                )
   405→            index = max(index + 1, cursor)
   406→            continue
   407→
   408→        index += 1
   409→
   410→
   411→def _detect_dead_useeffects(filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> None:
   412→    """Find useEffect calls with empty/whitespace/comment-only bodies."""
   413→    for line_no, line in enumerate(lines):
   414→        stripped = line.strip()
   415→        if not re.match(r"(?:React\.)?useEffect\s*\(\s*\(\s*\)\s*=>\s*\{", stripped):
   416→            continue
   417→
   418→        paren_depth = 0
   419→        end_line = None
   420→        for cursor in range(line_no, min(line_no + 30, len(lines))):
   421→            for _, ch, in_string in scan_code(lines[cursor]):
   422→                if in_string:
   423→                    continue
   424→                if ch == "(":
   425→                    paren_depth += 1
   426→                elif ch == ")":
   427→                    paren_depth -= 1
   428→                    if paren_depth <= 0:
   429→                        end_line = cursor
   430→                        break
   431→            if end_line is not None:
   432→                break
   433→
   434→        if end_line is None:
   435→            continue
   436→
   437→        text = "\n".join(lines[line_no:end_line + 1])
   438→        arrow_pos = text.find("=>")
   439→        if arrow_pos == -1:
   440→            continue
   441→        brace_pos = text.find("{", arrow_pos)
   442→        if brace_pos == -1:
   443→            continue
   444→
   445→        depth = 0
   446→        body_end = None
   447→        for idx, ch, in_string in scan_code(text, brace_pos):
   448→            if in_string:
   449→                continue
   450→            if ch == "{":
   451→                depth += 1
   452→            elif ch == "}":
   453→                depth -= 1
   454→                if depth == 0:
   455→                    body_end = idx
   456→                    break
   457→        if body_end is None:
   458→            continue
   459→
   460→        body = text[brace_pos + 1:body_end]
   461→        if _strip_ts_comments(body).strip() == "":
   462→            smell_counts["dead_useeffect"].append(
   463→                {"file": filepath, "line": line_no + 1, "content": stripped[:100]}
   464→            )
   465→
   466→
   467→def _script_is_documented(readme_text: str, script_name: str) -> bool:
   468→    escaped = re.escape(script_name)
   469→    command_patterns = [
   470→        rf"\bnpm\s+(?:run\s+)?{escaped}\b",
   471→        rf"\bpnpm\s+{escaped}\b",
   472→        rf"\byarn\s+{escaped}\b",
   473→        rf"\bbun\s+run\s+{escaped}\b",
   474→    ]
   475→    if any(re.search(pattern, readme_text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) for pattern in command_patterns):
   476→        return True
   477→    return bool(re.search(rf"`{escaped}`", readme_text))
   478→
   479→
   480→def _detect_non_ts_asset_smells(path: Path, smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> int:
   481→    """Scan adjacent non-TS assets (CSS/docs) for common repo-health smells."""
   482→    scanned_files = 0
   483→    css_files = find_source_files(path, [".css", ".scss", ".sass", ".less"])
   484→    scanned_files += len(css_files)
   485→
   486→    for filepath in css_files:
   487→        try:
   488→            full = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   489→            content = full.read_text()
   490→            lines = content.splitlines()
   491→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   492→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"read stylesheet smell candidate {filepath}", exc)
   493→            continue
   494→
   495→        if len(lines) >= 300:
   496→            smell_counts["css_monolith"].append(
   497→                {
   498→                    "file": filepath,
   499→                    "line": 1,
   500→                    "content": f"{len(lines)} LOC stylesheet",
   501→                }
   502→            )
   503→
   504→        important_count = content.count("!important")
   505→        if important_count >= 8:
   506→            first_line = next(
   507→                (idx + 1 for idx, line in enumerate(lines) if "!important" in line),
   508→                1,
   509→            )
   510→            smell_counts["css_important_overuse"].append(
   511→                {
   512→                    "file": filepath,
   513→                    "line": first_line,
   514→                    "content": f"{important_count} !important declarations",
   515→                }
   516→            )
   517→
   518→    readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "README.md"
   519→    package_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "package.json"
   520→    if not readme_path.is_file() or not package_path.is_file():
   521→        return scanned_files
   522→
   523→    scanned_files += 1
   524→    try:
   525→        readme_text = readme_path.read_text()
   526→        package_payload = json.loads(package_path.read_text())
   527→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   528→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "read package/readme for docs drift smell", exc)
   529→        return scanned_files
   530→
   531→    scripts = package_payload.get("scripts")
   532→    if not isinstance(scripts, dict):
   533→        return scanned_files
   534→
   535→    key_scripts = [
   536→        script for script in ("dev", "build", "test", "lint", "typecheck") if script in scripts
   537→    ]
   538→    if len(key_scripts) < 2:
   539→        return scanned_files
   540→
   541→    missing = [script for script in key_scripts if not _script_is_documented(readme_text, script)]
   542→    if len(missing) >= 2:
   543→        smell_counts["docs_scripts_drift"].append(
   544→            {
   545→                "file": "README.md",
   546→                "line": 1,
   547→                "content": f"Missing script docs: {', '.join(missing[:5])}",
   548→            }
   549→        )
   550→    return scanned_files
   551→
   552→
   553→def detect_smells(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   554→    """Detect TypeScript/React code smell patterns across the codebase.
   555→
   556→    Returns (entries, total_files_checked).
   557→    """
   558→    checks = TS_SMELL_CHECKS
   559→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {s["id"]: [] for s in checks}
   560→    files = find_ts_files(path)
   561→
   562→    for filepath in files:
   563→        if "node_modules" in filepath or ".d.ts" in filepath:
   564→            continue
   565→        try:
   566→            p = (
   567→                Path(filepath)
   568→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   569→                else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   570→            )
   571→            content = p.read_text()
   572→            lines = content.splitlines()
   573→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   574→            log_best_effort_failure(
   575→                logger, f"read TypeScript smell candidate {filepath}", exc
   576→            )
   577→            continue
   578→
   579→        # Build line state for string/comment filtering
   580→        line_state = _build_ts_line_state(lines)
   581→
   582→        # Regex-based smells
   583→        for check in checks:
   584→            if check["pattern"] is None:
   585→                continue
   586→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   587→                # Skip lines inside block comments or template literals
   588→                if i in line_state:
   589→                    continue
   590→                m = re.search(check["pattern"], line)
   591→                if not m:
   592→                    continue
   593→                # Check if match is inside a single-line string or comment
   594→                if _ts_match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   595→                    continue
   596→                # Skip URLs assigned to module-level constants
   597→                if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   598→                    r"^(?:export\s+)?(?:const|let|var)\s+[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=",
   599→                    line.strip(),
   600→                ):
   601→                    continue
   602→                smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   603→                    {
   604→                        "file": filepath,
   605→                        "line": i + 1,
   606→                        "content": line.strip()[:100],
   607→                    }
   608→                )
   609→
   610→        # Multi-line smell helpers (brace-tracked)
   611→        _detect_async_no_await(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   612→        _detect_error_no_throw(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   613→        _detect_empty_if_chains(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   614→        _detect_dead_useeffects(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   615→        _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   616→        _detect_monster_functions(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   617→        _detect_dead_functions(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   618→        _detect_window_globals(filepath, lines, line_state, smell_counts)
   619→        _detect_catch_return_default(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   620→        _detect_switch_no_default(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   621→        _detect_nested_closures(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   622→        _detect_high_cyclomatic_complexity(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   623→
   624→    non_ts_files = _detect_non_ts_asset_smells(path, smell_counts)
   625→
   626→    # Build summary entries sorted by severity then count
   627→    severity_order = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
   628→    entries = []
   629→    for check in checks:
   630→        matches = smell_counts[check["id"]]
   631→        if matches:
   632→            entries.append(
   633→                {
   634→                    "id": check["id"],
   635→                    "label": check["label"],
   636→                    "severity": check["severity"],
   637→                    "count": len(matches),
   638→                    "files": len(set(m["file"] for m in matches)),
   639→                    "matches": matches[:50],
   640→                }
   641→            )
   642→    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (severity_order.get(e["severity"], 9), -e["count"]))
   643→    return entries, len(files) + non_ts_files
   644→
```

```
1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    add_to_cluster,
    13→    create_cluster,
    14→    delete_cluster,
    15→    load_plan,
    16→    move_cluster,
    17→    remove_from_cluster,
    18→    save_plan,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    plan = load_plan()
    26→    try:
    27→        create_cluster(plan, name, description)
    28→    except ValueError as ex:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    30→        return
    31→    save_plan(plan)
    32→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    37→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    41→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    42→
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    45→    if not finding_ids:
    46→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    47→        return
    48→    try:
    49→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    50→    except ValueError as ex:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    52→        return
    53→    save_plan(plan)
    54→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    59→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    63→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    64→
    65→    plan = load_plan()
    66→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    67→    if not finding_ids:
    68→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→    try:
    71→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    72→    except ValueError as ex:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    74→        return
    75→    save_plan(plan)
    76→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    80→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    81→    plan = load_plan()
    82→    try:
    83→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    84→    except ValueError as ex:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    86→        return
    87→    save_plan(plan)
    88→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    94→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    95→
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→
    98→    offset: int | None = None
    99→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   100→        try:
   101→            offset = int(target)
   102→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   103→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   104→            return
   105→        target = None
   106→
   107→    try:
   108→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   109→    except ValueError as ex:
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   111→        return
   112→    save_plan(plan)
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→    if not clusters:
   121→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   122→        return
   123→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   124→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   127→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   130→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   131→
   132→
   133→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   134→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   135→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   136→    dispatch = {
   137→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   138→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   139→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   140→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   141→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   142→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   143→    }
   144→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   145→    if handler is None:
   146→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   147→        return
   148→    handler(args)
   149→
   150→
   151→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   152→
```

```
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )

   394→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   395→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   396→    for lang_name in langs:
   397→        try:
   398→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   399→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   400→            fixer_names = []
   401→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   402→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   403→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   404→    return fixer_help_lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   408→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   409→        "fix",
   410→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   411→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   412→    )
   413→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   414→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
   415→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
   416→    p_fix.add_argument(
   417→        "--dry-run",
   418→        action="store_true",
   419→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   420→    )
   421→
   422→
   423→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   424→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   425→        "plan", help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note"
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    13→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    14→
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Grouping key computation
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    21→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    22→
    23→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    24→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    25→    """
    26→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    27→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    28→    if kind:
    29→        return kind
    30→
    31→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    32→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    33→    parts = fid.split("::")
    34→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    35→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    36→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    37→        candidate = parts[-1]
    38→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    39→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    40→            return candidate
    41→    return None
    42→
    43→
    44→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    45→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    46→
    47→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    48→    """
    49→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    50→
    51→    if meta is None:
    52→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    53→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    54→
    55→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    56→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    57→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    58→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    59→        if dimension:
    60→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    61→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    62→
    63→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    64→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    65→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    66→    ):
    67→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    68→        if file:
    69→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    70→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    71→
    72→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    74→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    75→        if subtype:
    76→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    77→
    78→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    79→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    80→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    81→
    82→    # Everything else → by detector
    83→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    84→
    85→
    86→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    87→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    88→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    89→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    90→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
    91→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
    92→    # detector::security → auto/security
    93→    parts = key.split("::")
    94→    if len(parts) == 2:
    95→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    96→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
    97→        if prefix_type == "review":
    98→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
    99→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   100→    if len(parts) == 3:
   101→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   102→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   103→
   104→
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→# Description and action generation
   107→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108→
   109→def _generate_description(
   110→    cluster_name: str,
   111→    members: list[dict],
   112→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   113→    subtype: str | None,
   114→) -> str:
   115→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   116→    count = len(members)
   117→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   118→
   119→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   120→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   121→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   122→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   123→
   124→    if detector == "structural":
   125→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   126→        if len(files) == 1:
   127→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   128→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   129→
   130→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   131→    if subtype:
   132→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   133→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   134→
   135→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   136→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   137→
   138→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   139→
   140→
   141→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   142→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   143→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   144→        return None
   145→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   146→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   147→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   148→        return fixer_name
   149→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   150→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   151→        if subtype in fixer:
   152→            return fixer
   153→    return None
   154→
   155→
   156→def _generate_action(
   157→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   158→    subtype: str | None,
   159→) -> str | None:
   160→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata."""
   161→    if meta is None:
   162→        return None
   163→
   164→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   165→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   166→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   167→        if matched_fixer:
   168→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   169→        # Subtype has no fixer — don't fall through to generic detector guidance
   170→        # since it would be misleading (e.g. "dead useEffect" for cyclomatic complexity)
   171→        return None
   172→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   173→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   174→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   175→
   176→    if meta.tool == "move":
   177→        return "desloppify move"
   178→
   179→    # Only show guidance for detectors without subtypes — for subtyped detectors
   180→    # the generic guidance is too broad to be useful
   181→    if meta.guidance and not subtype:
   182→        return meta.guidance
   183→
   184→    return None
   185→
   186→
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→# Main algorithm
   189→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→
   191→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   192→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   193→
   194→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   195→    """
   196→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   197→    changes = 0
   198→
   199→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   200→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   201→
   202→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   203→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   204→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   205→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   206→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   207→
   208→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   209→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   210→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   211→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   212→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   213→            continue
   214→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   215→            continue
   216→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   217→            continue
   218→
   219→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   220→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   221→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   222→        if key is None:
   223→            continue
   224→
   225→        groups[key].append(fid)
   226→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   227→
   228→    # Drop singleton groups
   229→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   230→
   231→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   232→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   233→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   234→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   235→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   236→            if ck:
   237→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   238→
   239→    now = utc_now()
   240→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   241→
   242→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   243→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   244→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   245→
   246→        # Representative finding for metadata
   247→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   248→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   249→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   250→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   251→
   252→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   253→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   254→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   255→
   256→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   257→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   258→
   259→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   260→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   261→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   262→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   263→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   264→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   265→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   266→                if new_ids:
   267→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   268→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   269→                    changes += 1
   270→            else:
   271→                # Replace membership wholesale
   272→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   273→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   274→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   275→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   276→                    cluster["description"] = description
   277→                    cluster["action"] = action
   278→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   279→                    changes += 1
   280→        else:
   281→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   282→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   283→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   284→                # Append a disambiguator
   285→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   286→
   287→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   288→                "name": cluster_name,
   289→                "description": description,
   290→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   291→                "created_at": now,
   292→                "updated_at": now,
   293→                "auto": True,
   294→                "cluster_key": key,
   295→                "action": action,
   296→                "user_modified": False,
   297→            }
   298→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   299→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   300→            changes += 1
   301→
   302→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   303→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   304→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   305→        for fid in member_ids:
   306→            if fid not in overrides:
   307→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   308→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   309→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   310→
   311→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   312→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   313→        cluster = clusters[name]
   314→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   315→            continue
   316→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   317→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   318→            continue
   319→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   320→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   321→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   322→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   323→            if alive:
   324→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   325→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   326→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   327→                    changes += 1
   328→                continue
   329→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   330→        # Delete stale cluster
   331→        del clusters[name]
   332→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   333→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   334→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   335→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   336→                override["cluster"] = None
   337→                override["updated_at"] = now
   338→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   339→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    plan["updated"] = now
   343→    return changes
   344→
   345→
   346→__all__ = [
   347→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   348→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   349→]
   350→
```

```
   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)

   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))

   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))

   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(

   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True

   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,

   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→

   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '

   432→    )
   433→    print(

   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'

   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
   248→) -> Cluster:
   249→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   250→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   251→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   252→        raise ValueError(
   253→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   254→        )
   255→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   256→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   257→    now = utc_now()
   258→    cluster: Cluster = {
   259→        "name": name,
   260→        "description": description,
   261→        "finding_ids": [],
   262→        "created_at": now,
   263→        "updated_at": now,
   264→        "auto": False,
   265→        "cluster_key": "",
   266→        "action": None,
   267→        "user_modified": False,
   268→    }
   269→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   270→    return cluster
   271→
   272→
   273→def add_to_cluster(
   274→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   275→) -> int:
   276→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   279→    if cluster is None:
   280→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   281→
   282→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   283→    count = 0
   284→    now = utc_now()
   285→    for fid in finding_ids:
   286→        if fid not in member_ids:
   287→            member_ids.append(fid)
   288→            count += 1
   289→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   290→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   291→        if fid not in overrides:
   292→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   293→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   294→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   295→
   296→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   297→    return count
   298→
   299→
   300→def remove_from_cluster(
   301→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   302→) -> int:
   303→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   304→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   305→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   306→    if cluster is None:
   307→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   308→
   309→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   310→    now = utc_now()
   311→    count = 0
   312→    for fid in finding_ids:
   313→        if fid in member_ids:
   314→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   315→            count += 1
   316→        # Clear cluster from override
   317→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   318→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   319→            override["cluster"] = None
   320→            override["updated_at"] = now
   321→
   322→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   323→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   324→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   325→
   326→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   327→    return count
   328→
   329→
   330→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   331→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   332→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   333→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   334→    if cluster is None:
   335→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   336→
   337→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   338→    now = utc_now()
   339→    for fid in orphaned:
   340→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   341→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   342→            override["cluster"] = None
   343→            override["updated_at"] = now
   344→
   345→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   346→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   347→
   348→    return orphaned
   349→
   350→
   351→def move_cluster(
   352→    plan: PlanModel,
   353→    cluster_name: str,
   354→    position: str,
   355→    target: str | None = None,
   356→    offset: int | None = None,
   357→) -> int:
   358→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   359→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   360→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   361→    if cluster is None:
   362→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   363→
   364→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   365→    if not member_ids:
   366→        return 0
   367→
   368→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   369→
   370→
   371→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   372→# Focus
   373→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   374→
   375→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   376→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   377→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   378→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   379→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   380→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   381→
   382→
   383→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   384→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   385→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   386→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   387→
   388→
   389→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   390→# Reset
   391→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   392→
   393→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   394→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   395→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   396→    plan.clear()
   397→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   398→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   399→        plan[k] = v
   400→    plan["created"] = created
   401→
   402→
   403→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   404→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   405→
   406→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   407→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   408→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   409→    """
   410→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   411→    found = 0
   412→
   413→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   414→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   415→    for fid in finding_ids:
   416→        was_present = False
   417→        if fid in order:
   418→            order.remove(fid)
   419→            was_present = True
   420→        if fid in skipped:
   421→            skipped.pop(fid)
   422→            was_present = True
   423→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   424→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   425→            if fid in ids:
   426→                ids.remove(fid)
   427→                was_present = True
   428→        if was_present:
   429→            found += 1
   430→
   431→    return found
   432→
   433→
   434→__all__ = [
   435→    "add_to_cluster",
   436→    "annotate_finding",
   437→    "clear_focus",
   438→    "create_cluster",
   439→    "delete_cluster",
   440→    "describe_finding",
   441→    "move_cluster",
   442→    "move_items",
   443→    "purge_ids",
   444→    "remove_from_cluster",
   445→    "reset_plan",
   446→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   447→    "set_focus",
   448→    "skip_items",
   449→    "unskip_items",
   450→]
   451→
```

```
1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    85→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    86→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    87→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    92→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    93→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    supported: set[str] | None,
    95→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    96→    state: StateModel,
    97→) -> None:
    98→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
```
