```
1→"""Finding factory functions — normalize raw detector output into Finding dicts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import Tier
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    10→from desloppify.state import Finding, make_finding
    11→
    12→
    13→def make_unused_findings(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Finding]:
    14→    """Transform raw unused-detector entries into normalized findings.
    15→
    16→    Shared by both Python and TypeScript unused phases.
    17→    """
    18→    results = []
    19→    for e in entries:
    20→        tier = 1 if e["category"] == "imports" else 2
    21→        results.append(
    22→            make_finding(
    23→                "unused",
    24→                e["file"],
    25→                e["name"],
    26→                tier=tier,
    27→                confidence="high",
    28→                summary=f"Unused {e['category']}: {e['name']}",
    29→                detail={"line": e["line"], "category": e["category"]},
    30→            )
    31→        )
    32→    stderr_fn(f"         {len(entries)} instances -> {len(results)} findings")
    33→    return results
    34→
    35→
    36→def make_dupe_findings(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Finding]:
    37→    """Transform clustered duplicate entries into normalized findings.
    38→
    39→    Each entry represents a cluster of similar functions. One finding per cluster.
    40→    """
    41→    results = []
    42→    for e in entries:
    43→        a, b = e["fn_a"], e["fn_b"]
    44→        if a["loc"] < 10 and b["loc"] < 10:
    45→            continue
    46→        cluster_size = e.get("cluster_size", 2)
    47→        pair = sorted([(a["file"], a["name"]), (b["file"], b["name"])])
    48→        name = f"{pair[0][1]}::{rel(pair[1][0])}::{pair[1][1]}"
    49→        tier = 2 if e["kind"] == "exact" else 3
    50→        conf = "high" if e["kind"] == "exact" else "low"
    51→        kind_label = "Exact" if e["kind"] == "exact" else "Near"
    52→        if cluster_size > 2:
    53→            summary = (
    54→                f"{kind_label} dupe cluster ({cluster_size} functions, "
    55→                f"{e['similarity']:.0%} similar): "
    56→                f"{a['name']} ({rel(a['file'])}:{a['line']}), "
    57→                f"{b['name']} ({rel(b['file'])}:{b['line']}), ..."
    58→            )
    59→        else:
    60→            summary = (
    61→                f"{kind_label} dupe: "
    62→                f"{a['name']} ({rel(a['file'])}:{a['line']}) <-> "
    63→                f"{b['name']} ({rel(b['file'])}:{b['line']}) [{e['similarity']:.0%}]"
    64→            )
    65→        results.append(
    66→            make_finding(
    67→                "dupes",
    68→                pair[0][0],
    69→                name,
    70→                tier=tier,
    71→                confidence=conf,
    72→                summary=summary,
    73→                detail={
    74→                    "fn_a": a,
    75→                    "fn_b": b,
    76→                    "similarity": e["similarity"],
    77→                    "kind": e["kind"],
    78→                    "cluster_size": cluster_size,
    79→                    "cluster": e.get("cluster", [a, b]),
    80→                },
    81→            )
    82→        )
    83→    suppressed = sum(
    84→        1 for e in entries if e["fn_a"]["loc"] < 10 and e["fn_b"]["loc"] < 10
    85→    )
    86→    stderr_fn(f"         {len(entries)} clusters, {suppressed} suppressed (<10 LOC)")
    87→    return results
    88→
    89→
    90→def make_single_use_findings(
    91→    entries: list[dict],
    92→    get_area,
    93→    *,
    94→    loc_range: tuple[int, int] = (50, 200),
    95→    suppress_colocated: bool = True,
    96→    skip_dir_names: set[str] | None = None,
    97→    stderr_fn,
    98→) -> list[Finding]:
    99→    """Filter and normalize single-use entries into findings.
   100→
   101→    Suppresses entries within the LOC range (they're appropriately-sized abstractions),
   102→    entries co-located with their sole importer, and entries in skip_dir_names
   103→    directories (e.g., commands/ -- CLI modules are single-use by design).
   104→    """
   105→    results = []
   106→    colocated_suppressed = 0
   107→    lo, hi = loc_range
   108→    for e in entries:
   109→        if lo <= e["loc"] <= hi:
   110→            continue
   111→        # Skip files in directories that are single-use by design (e.g., commands/)
   112→        if skip_dir_names:
   113→            parts = Path(e["file"]).parts
   114→            if any(p in skip_dir_names for p in parts):
   115→                continue
   116→        if suppress_colocated and get_area:
   117→            src_area = get_area(rel(e["file"]))
   118→            imp_area = get_area(e["sole_importer"])
   119→            if src_area == imp_area:
   120→                colocated_suppressed += 1
   121→                continue
   122→        results.append(
   123→            make_finding(
   124→                "single_use",
   125→                e["file"],
   126→                "",
   127→                tier=3,
   128→                confidence="medium",
   129→                summary=f"Single-use ({e['loc']} LOC): only imported by {e['sole_importer']}",
   130→                detail={"loc": e["loc"], "sole_importer": e["sole_importer"]},
   131→            )
   132→        )
   133→    suppressed = len(entries) - len(results)
   134→    coloc_note = f", {colocated_suppressed} co-located" if colocated_suppressed else ""
   135→    stderr_fn(
   136→        f"         single-use: {len(entries)} found, {suppressed} suppressed "
   137→        f"({lo}-{hi} LOC{coloc_note})"
   138→    )
   139→    return results
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_cycle_findings(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Finding]:
   143→    """Normalize import cycles into findings."""
   144→    results = []
   145→    for cy in entries:
   146→        cycle_files = [rel(f) for f in cy["files"]]
   147→        name = "::".join(cycle_files[:4])
   148→        if len(cycle_files) > 4:
   149→            name += f"::+{len(cycle_files) - 4}"
   150→        tier = 3 if cy["length"] <= 3 else 4
   151→        results.append(
   152→            make_finding(
   153→                "cycles",
   154→                cy["files"][0],
   155→                name,
   156→                tier=tier,
   157→                confidence="high",
   158→                summary=f"Import cycle ({cy['length']} files): "
   159→                + " -> ".join(cycle_files[:5])
   160→                + (f" -> +{len(cycle_files) - 5}" if len(cycle_files) > 5 else ""),
   161→                detail={"files": cycle_files, "length": cy["length"]},
   162→            )
   163→        )
   164→    if entries:
   165→        stderr_fn(f"         cycles: {len(entries)} import cycles")
   166→    return results
   167→
   168→
   169→def make_orphaned_findings(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Finding]:
   170→    """Normalize orphaned file entries into findings."""
   171→    results = []
   172→    for e in entries:
   173→        results.append(
   174→            make_finding(
   175→                "orphaned",
   176→                e["file"],
   177→                "",
   178→                tier=3,
   179→                confidence="medium",
   180→                summary=f"Orphaned file ({e['loc']} LOC): zero importers, not an entry point",
   181→                detail={"loc": e["loc"]},
   182→            )
   183→        )
   184→    if entries:
   185→        stderr_fn(f"         orphaned: {len(entries)} files with zero importers")
   186→    return results
   187→
   188→
   189→SMELL_TIER_MAP = {"high": Tier.QUICK_FIX, "medium": Tier.JUDGMENT, "low": Tier.JUDGMENT}
   190→
   191→
   192→def make_smell_findings(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Finding]:
   193→    """Group smell entries by file and assign tiers from severity.
   194→
   195→    Input: list of smell dicts from detect_smells, each with id/label/severity/matches.
   196→    Output: findings grouped per (file, smell_id).
   197→    """
   198→    results = []
   199→    for e in entries:
   200→        by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
```

Excellent! I have all five files. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the cluster command patterns and implementation structure.

## Complete Implementation Patterns for `plan cluster show <name>`

### 1. **Cluster Handlers Pattern** (`cluster_handlers.py`)

The file demonstrates a clear pattern for cluster subcommands:

**Pattern Structure:**
```python
def _cmd_cluster_<action>(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    # 1. Extract arguments using getattr(args, "arg_name", default)
    arg_value: str = getattr(args, "arg_name", "")
    
    # 2. Load plan (when needed)
    plan = load_plan()
    
    # 3. Perform operation (call engine function)
    result = operation_func(plan, ...)
    
    # 4. Save plan on success
    save_plan(plan)
    
    # 5. Print colorized output
    print(colorize(f"  Message", "color"))
```

**Existing cluster subcommands with their patterns:**
- `create`: Takes `cluster_name` + optional `description`. Calls `create_cluster()`. Prints success/error.
- `add`: Takes `cluster_name` + `patterns`. Resolves patterns to IDs. Calls `add_to_cluster()`. Prints count added.
- `remove`: Takes `cluster_name` + `patterns`. Resolves patterns to IDs. Calls `remove_from_cluster()`. Prints count removed.
- `delete`: Takes `cluster_name`. Calls `delete_cluster()`. Prints count orphaned.
- `move`: Takes `cluster_name` + `position` + optional `target`/`offset`. Calls `move_cluster()`. Prints count moved.
- `list`: Iterates clusters directly. Prints name, member_count, description, auto flag, focused marker.

**Dispatch pattern:**
```python
def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
    dispatch = {
        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
        # ... etc
    }
    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
    if handler is None:
        _cmd_cluster_list(args)  # Default to list
        return
    handler(args)
```

---

### 2. **Parser Registration** (`parser_groups_admin.py`, lines 535-568)

**Parser structure for cluster subcommands:**
```python
# plan cluster ...
p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser("cluster", help="Manage finding clusters")
cluster_sub = p_cluster.add_subparsers(dest="cluster_action")

# Each subcommand registered:
p_cc = cluster_sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a cluster")
p_cc.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name (slug)")
p_cc.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")

p_ca = cluster_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add findings to a cluster")
p_ca.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
p_ca.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding patterns")
```

**For `plan cluster show <name>`**, you would add:
```python
# plan cluster show <name>
p_cs = cluster_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show cluster details")
p_cs.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
```

---

### 3. **Rendering Patterns** (`next_parts/render.py`, lines 100-145)

The `_render_cluster_item()` function demonstrates the pattern for cluster display:

```python
def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
    type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    
    # Header
    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    
    # Summary
    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    
    # File distribution (compact or summarized)
    members = item.get("members", [])
    if members:
        from collections import Counter
        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
        else:
            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
    
    # Sample member IDs
    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
    for m in members[:3]:
        print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
    if len(members) > 3:
        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
    
    # Action command
    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
    if primary_command:
        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
    
    # Resolution hints
    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}"...', "dim"))
    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name}...", "dim"))
```

**Reusable rendering elements for `plan cluster show`:**
- `colorize()` for styling (bold, cyan, dim, yellow, green, red)
- Separator line: `"─" * 60`
- File distribution via Counter
- Compact member list with "... and N more" pattern
- Action commands in cyan
- Helper hints in dim

---

### 4. **Plan Dispatch** (`cmd.py`, lines 64-119)

The dispatcher shows how cluster routing works:

```python
def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)

    if plan_action is None:
        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
        return

    if plan_action == "show":
        _cmd_plan_show(args)
        return

    # ... other single-level actions ...

    if plan_action == "cluster":
        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)  # Delegate to cluster_handlers
        return
```

**Pattern:** The top-level `plan_action` dispatcher forwards to `cmd_cluster_dispatch()` which then reads `cluster_action` from args.

---

### 5. **Engine Operations** (`engine/_plan/operations.py`, lines 246-369)

**Cluster data structure (Cluster TypedDict from schema):**
```python
class Cluster(TypedDict):
    name: str
    description: str | None
    finding_ids: list[str]
    created_at: str (ISO8601)
    updated_at: str (ISO8601)
    auto: bool
    cluster_key: str
    action: str | None
    user_modified: bool
```

**Key operations:**
- `create_cluster(plan, name, description)` → returns `Cluster`, raises ValueError if exists
- `add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)` → returns int (count added)
- `remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)` → returns int (count removed)
- `delete_cluster(plan, name)` → returns list[str] (orphaned IDs)
- `move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target, offset)` → returns int (count moved)

**For a `show` operation**, you would need:
```python
def get_cluster_details(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> Cluster | None:
    """Retrieve cluster details by name."""
    return plan["clusters"].get(name)
```

```
1→"""DictKeyVisitor — AST visitor tracking dict key writes/reads per scope."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import ast
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors.dict_keys import (
     8→    _BULK_READ_METHODS,
     9→    _CONFIG_NAMES,
    10→    TrackedDict,
    11→    _get_name,
    12→    _get_str_key,
    13→    _is_singular_plural,
    14→    _levenshtein,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _mark_returned_or_passed(visitor: DictKeyVisitor, node: ast.expr) -> None:
    19→    """Mark a tracked dict expression as escaped via return/call boundary."""
    20→    if isinstance(node, ast.Tuple):
    21→        for elt in node.elts:
    22→            _mark_returned_or_passed(visitor, elt)
    23→        return
    24→    name = _get_name(node)
    25→    if not name:
    26→        return
    27→    tracked = visitor._get_tracked(name)
    28→    if tracked:
    29→        tracked.returned_or_passed = True
    30→
    31→
    32→def _record_call_interactions(visitor: DictKeyVisitor, node: ast.Call) -> None:
    33→    """Update tracked dict read/write metadata from a call expression."""
    34→    if isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
    35→        name = _get_name(node.func.value)
    36→        method = node.func.attr
    37→        if name:
    38→            tracked = visitor._get_tracked(name)
    39→            if tracked:
    40→                if method in ("get", "pop", "__getitem__", "__contains__"):
    41→                    if node.args:
    42→                        key = _get_str_key(node.args[0])
    43→                        if key:
    44→                            tracked.reads[key].append(node.lineno)
    45→                        else:
    46→                            tracked.has_dynamic_key = True
    47→                elif method == "setdefault":
    48→                    if node.args:
    49→                        key = _get_str_key(node.args[0])
    50→                        if key:
    51→                            tracked.reads[key].append(node.lineno)
    52→                            tracked.writes[key].append(node.lineno)
    53→                        else:
    54→                            tracked.has_dynamic_key = True
    55→                elif method == "update":
    56→                    if node.args and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.Dict):
    57→                        for key_node in node.args[0].keys:
    58→                            key = _get_str_key(key_node) if key_node else None
    59→                            if key:
    60→                                tracked.writes[key].append(node.lineno)
    61→                            elif key_node is None:
    62→                                tracked.has_dynamic_key = True
    63→                    for kw in node.keywords:
    64→                        if kw.arg:
    65→                            tracked.writes[kw.arg].append(node.lineno)
    66→                        else:
    67→                            tracked.has_dynamic_key = True
    68→                elif method in _BULK_READ_METHODS:
    69→                    tracked.bulk_read = True
    70→
    71→    for arg in node.args:
    72→        _mark_returned_or_passed(visitor, arg)
    73→    for kw in node.keywords:
    74→        if kw.arg is None:
    75→            name = _get_name(kw.value)
    76→            if name:
    77→                tracked = visitor._get_tracked(name)
    78→                if tracked:
    79→                    tracked.has_star_unpack = True
    80→        else:
    81→            _mark_returned_or_passed(visitor, kw.value)
    82→
    83→
    84→def _analyze_scope_findings(
    85→    visitor: DictKeyVisitor,
    86→    scope: dict[str, TrackedDict],
    87→    func_name: str,
    88→    *,
    89→    is_class: bool = False,
    90→) -> list[dict]:
    91→    """Analyze a completed scope and return dict-key findings."""
    92→    findings: list[dict] = []
    93→    for tracked in scope.values():
    94→        if not tracked.locally_created:
    95→            continue
    96→
    97→        suppress_dead = (
    98→            tracked.returned_or_passed
    99→            or tracked.has_dynamic_key
   100→            or tracked.has_star_unpack
   101→            or tracked.bulk_read
   102→            or any(
   103→                tracked.name.lower().endswith(name) or tracked.name.lower() == name
   104→                for name in _CONFIG_NAMES
   105→            )
   106→            or (not is_class and func_name in ("__init__", "setUp", "setup"))
   107→            or sum(len(lines) for lines in tracked.writes.values()) < 3
   108→        )
   109→
   110→        written_keys = set(tracked.writes.keys())
   111→        read_keys = set(tracked.reads.keys())
   112→
   113→        dead_keys = written_keys - read_keys
   114→        if not suppress_dead:
   115→            for key in sorted(dead_keys):
   116→                line = tracked.writes[key][0]
   117→                findings.append(
   118→                    {
   119→                        "file": visitor.filepath,
   120→                        "kind": "dead_write",
   121→                        "variable": tracked.name,
   122→                        "key": key,
   123→                        "line": line,
   124→                        "func": func_name,
   125→                        "tier": 3,
   126→                        "confidence": "medium",
   127→                        "summary": (
   128→                            f'Dict key "{key}" written to `{tracked.name}` '
   129→                            f"at line {line} but never read"
   130→                        ),
   131→                        "detail": f"in {func_name}()",
   132→                    }
   133→                )
   134→
   135→        phantom_keys = read_keys - written_keys
   136→        for key in sorted(phantom_keys):
   137→            line = tracked.reads[key][0]
   138→            findings.append(
   139→                {
   140→                    "file": visitor.filepath,
   141→                    "kind": "phantom_read",
   142→                    "variable": tracked.name,
   143→                    "key": key,
   144→                    "line": line,
   145→                    "func": func_name,
   146→                    "tier": 2,
   147→                    "confidence": "high",
   148→                    "summary": (
   149→                        f'Dict key "{key}" read at line {line} '
   150→                        f"but never written to `{tracked.name}`"
```

## Summary: Subtype-Specific Guidance Architecture

I've completed a thorough exploration of the codebase. Here's what I found about how subtypes work and what guidance already exists:

### 1. **Registry Entry for "smells"** (from `desloppify/core/registry.py` lines 84-92)

```python
"smells": DetectorMeta(
    "smells",
    "smells",
    "Code quality",
    "auto_fix",
    "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains",
    fixers=("dead-useeffect", "empty-if-chain"),
    needs_judgment=True,
),
```

**Key finding:** The current guidance is **generic and detector-level only** — "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" is just ONE example mentioning two specific subtypes. There's **NO subtype-specific guidance in the registry.**

### 2. **Dict_keys Detector (Comparative Example)**

The `dict_keys` detector entry (lines 206-214) also shows **generic guidance**:
```
"fix dict key mismatches — dead writes are likely dead code, schema drift suggests a typo or missed rename"
```

But dict_keys findings have a `kind` field in the detail that maps to:
- `"dead_write"` — written but never read
- `"phantom_read"` — read but never written
- `"near_miss"` — typo-like key name matches
- `"overwritten_key"` — key written multiple times

### 3. **How Subtypes Are Captured in Findings**

Smell findings use `detail.smell_id` field:
```python
{
    "id": "smells::desloppify/path.py::monster_function",
    "detector": "smells",
    "detail": {
        "smell_id": "monster_function",  # <-- The subtype
        "severity": "high",
        "count": 1,
        "lines": [73]
    }
}
```

Dict_keys findings use `kind` field in detail:
```python
{
    "id": "dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read",
    "detector": "dict_keys",
    "detail": {
        "kind": "phantom_read",  # <-- The subtype
        "variable": "config",
        "key": "api_token",
        ...
    }
}
```

### 4. **All Existing Smell Subtypes**

**Python (20 subtypes):**
- `annotation_quality`, `broad_except`, `dead_function`, `deferred_import`, `duplicate_constant`, `empty_except`, `global_keyword`, `hardcoded_url`, `high_cyclomatic_complexity`, `monster_function`, `mutable_class_var`, `nested_closure`, `noop_function`, `optional_param_sprawl`, `silent_except`, `subprocess_no_timeout`, `swallowed_error`, `unreachable_code`, `unsafe_file_write`, `unused_loop_var`

**TypeScript (17 subtypes):**
- `any_type`, `as_any_cast`, `async_no_await`, `console_error_no_throw`, `dead_function`, `hardcoded_color`, `hardcoded_url`, `magic_number`, `monster_function`, `non_null_assert`, `sort_no_comparator`, `switch_no_default`, `todo_fixme`, `ts_ignore`, `voided_symbol`, `window_global`, `workaround_tag`

### 5. **How `auto_cluster.py` Currently Handles Subtypes** (lines 141-184)

The file has a `_generate_action()` function that DOES handle subtypes but **only for fixers**:

```python
def _generate_action(meta, subtype):
    """Generate an action string from detector metadata."""
    if subtype and meta.fixers:
        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
        if matched_fixer:
            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
        return None
    ...
    if meta.guidance and not subtype:  # <-- Only shows guidance WITHOUT subtypes
        return meta.guidance
    return None
```

**Key insight:** When there's a subtype, the code **explicitly DOES NOT show the generic guidance** (line 181), returning `None` instead. This is intentional but leaves the user with no specific action guidance.

### 6. **Smell Details as Created** (from `finding_factories.py` lines 192-223)

Smell findings are created with minimal detail structure — only what's needed to identify the subtype:
```python
detail={
    "smell_id": e["id"],           # The subtype
    "severity": e["severity"],     # high/medium/low
    "count": len(matches),         # How many instances
    "lines": [m["line"] for m in matches[:10]],  # First 10 line numbers
}
```

---

## **What This Means for Subtype-Specific Guidance**

The architecture is already **prepared** to support subtype-specific guidance:

1. **Finding ID format supports it:** `detector::file::symbol::subtype`
2. **Detail extraction works:** `auto_cluster.py._extract_subtype()` can parse it
3. **Clustering already groups by subtype:** `typed::detector::subtype` keys
4. **Cluster descriptions are generated:** `_generate_description()` uses subtypes (line 131-133):
   ```python
   if subtype:
       label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
       return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   ```

**But guidance is missing:** There's no per-subtype guidance text anywhere in the codebase. The registry only has detector-level guidance (line 182 blocks it for subtypes).

---

## **Where Guidance Would Need to Go**

To implement subtype-specific guidance, you would need to:

1. **Extend registry.py:** Add a per-detector mapping of subtypes → guidance text, OR use a hook system like `on_detector_registered()` already exists
2. **Modify `_generate_action()` in auto_cluster.py:** Use subtype-specific guidance instead of returning `None`
3. **Follow the dict_keys pattern:** Use the `detail` dict's semantic field (`kind` for dict_keys, `smell_id` for smells)

The infrastructure is there — it's just that the guidance content doesn't exist yet.

```
1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any

     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    85→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    86→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    87→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    92→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    93→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    supported: set[str] | None,
    95→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    96→    state: StateModel,
    97→) -> None:
    98→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    99→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   100→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   101→            continue
   102→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   103→        if count == 0:
   104→            continue
   105→
   106→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   107→        available_fixers = [
   108→            fixer
   109→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   110→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   111→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   112→        ]
   113→        if not available_fixers:
   114→            actions.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   117→                    "detector": detector,
   118→                    "count": count,
   119→                    "description": (
   120→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   121→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   122→                    ),
   123→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   124→                    "impact": impact,
   125→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   126→                }
   127→            )
   128→            continue
   129→
   130→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   131→        actions.append(
   132→            {
   133→                "type": "auto_fix",
   134→                "detector": detector,
   135→                "count": count,
   136→                "description": (
   137→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   138→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   139→                ),
   140→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   141→                "impact": impact,
   142→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   143→            }
   144→        )
   145→
   146→
   147→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   148→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   149→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   150→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   151→) -> None:
   152→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
   153→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   154→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
   155→            continue
   156→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   157→        if count == 0:
   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   161→        actions.append(
   162→            {
   163→                "type": "reorganize",
   164→                "detector": detector,
   165→                "count": count,
   166→                "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}",
   167→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   168→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   169→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   170→            }
   171→        )
   172→
   173→
   174→def _build_refactor_entry(
   175→    detector: str,
   176→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   177→    count: int,
   178→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   179→) -> ActionItem:
   180→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   181→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   182→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   183→
   184→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   185→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   186→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   187→    elif detector == "review":
   188→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   189→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   190→        description = (
   191→            f"{count} review finding{suffix} need investigation — "
   192→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   193→        )
   194→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   195→    else:
   196→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   197→        description = f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}"
   198→
   199→    return {
   200→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "count": count,
   203→        "description": description,
   204→        "command": command,
   205→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   206→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def _append_refactor_actions(
   211→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   212→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   213→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   214→) -> None:
   215→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   216→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   217→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   218→            continue
   219→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   220→        if count == 0:
   221→            continue
   222→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   223→
   224→
   225→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   226→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   227→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   228→    if gap <= 2.0:
   229→        return
   230→    actions.append(
   231→        {
   232→            "type": "debt_review",
   233→            "detector": None,
   234→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   235→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   236→            "gap": gap,
   237→        }
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   242→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   243→    type_order = {
   244→        "issue_queue": 0,
   245→        "auto_fix": 1,
   246→        "reorganize": 2,
   247→        "refactor": 3,
   248→        "manual_fix": 4,
   249→        "debt_review": 5,
   250→    }
   251→    actions.sort(
   252→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   253→    )
   254→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   255→        action["priority"] = index
   256→    return actions
   257→
   258→
   259→def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
   260→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
   261→    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
   262→    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
   263→    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)
   264→
   265→    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
   266→    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   267→    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   268→    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
   269→
   270→    return _assign_priorities(actions)
   271→
```

```
1→"""Execution strategy engine for narrative lanes and parallelization hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
     9→    _DETECTOR_CASCADE,
    10→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    11→)
    12→
    13→
    14→def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    15→    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    16→    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    17→    for finding in findings.values():
    18→        if finding["status"] != "open" or finding.get("suppressed"):
    19→            continue
    20→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    21→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    22→            detector = "structural"
    23→        file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    24→        if not file_path:
    25→            by_detector.setdefault(detector, set())
    26→            continue
    27→        by_detector.setdefault(detector, set()).add(file_path)
    28→    return by_detector
    29→
    30→
    31→def compute_fixer_leverage(
    32→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    33→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    34→    phase: str,
    35→    _lang: str | None,
    36→) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    37→    """Estimate how much value automated fixers would deliver."""
    38→    auto_fixable = sum(
    39→        action.get("count", 0) for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    40→    )
    41→    total = sum(by_detector.values())
    42→    coverage = auto_fixable / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    43→    total_impact = sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in actions)
    44→    auto_impact = sum(
    45→        action.get("impact", 0)
    46→        for action in actions
    47→        if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    48→    )
    49→    impact_ratio = auto_impact / total_impact if total_impact > 0 else 0.0
    50→
    51→    if coverage == 0:
    52→        recommendation = "none"
    53→    elif coverage > 0.4 or impact_ratio > 0.3:
    54→        recommendation = "strong"
    55→    elif phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression") and coverage > 0.15:
    56→        recommendation = "strong"
    57→    elif coverage > 0.1:
    58→        recommendation = "moderate"
    59→    else:
    60→        recommendation = "none"
    61→
    62→    return {
    63→        "auto_fixable_count": auto_fixable,
    64→        "total_count": total,
    65→        "coverage": round(coverage, 3),
    66→        "impact_ratio": round(impact_ratio, 3),
    67→        "recommendation": recommendation,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _cleanup_lane_actions(actions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    72→    cascade_rank = {detector: idx for idx, detector in enumerate(_DETECTOR_CASCADE)}
    73→
    74→    def sort_key(action: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, float]:
    75→        detector = action.get("detector", "")
    76→        return (cascade_rank.get(detector, 99), -action.get("impact", 0))
    77→
    78→    return sorted(actions, key=sort_key)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _files_for_actions(
    82→    actions: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]]
    83→) -> set[str]:
    84→    files: set[str] = set()
    85→    for action in actions:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector")
    87→        if detector and detector in files_by_detector:
    88→            files |= files_by_detector[detector]
    89→    return files
    90→
    91→
    92→def _group_by_file_overlap(
    93→    action_files: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]],
    94→) -> list[list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]]]:
    95→    """Group actions whose file sets overlap using union-find."""
    96→    item_count = len(action_files)
    97→    if item_count == 0:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    parent = list(range(item_count))
   101→
   102→    def find(index: int) -> int:
   103→        while parent[index] != index:
   104→            parent[index] = parent[parent[index]]
   105→            index = parent[index]
   106→        return index
   107→
   108→    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
   109→        left_root, right_root = find(left), find(right)
   110→        if left_root != right_root:
   111→            parent[left_root] = right_root
   112→
   113→    for left in range(item_count):
   114→        for right in range(left + 1, item_count):
   115→            if action_files[left][1] & action_files[right][1]:
   116→                union(left, right)
   117→
   118→    grouped_indices: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
   119→    for index in range(item_count):
   120→        grouped_indices.setdefault(find(index), []).append(index)
   121→
   122→    return [
   123→        [action_files[index] for index in indices]
   124→        for indices in grouped_indices.values()
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def _refactor_lanes(
   129→    refactor_actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   130→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   131→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   132→    """Split refactor work into independent lanes by file overlap."""
   133→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   134→    action_files = [
   135→        (action, files_by_detector.get(action.get("detector"), set()))
   136→        for action in refactor_actions
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    test_coverage_actions = [
   140→        (action, files)
   141→        for action, files in action_files
   142→        if action.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
   143→    ]
   144→    other_actions = [
   145→        (action, files)
   146→        for action, files in action_files
   147→        if action.get("detector") != "test_coverage"
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    groups = _group_by_file_overlap(other_actions)
   151→    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
   152→        lane_name = f"refactor_{index}" if len(groups) > 1 else "refactor"
   153→        lanes[lane_name] = {
   154→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in group],
   155→            "file_count": len(
   156→                _files_for_actions((action for action, _ in group), files_by_detector)
   157→            ),
   158→            "total_impact": round(
   159→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in group), 1
   160→            ),
   161→            "automation": "manual",
   162→            "run_first": False,
   163→        }
   164→
   165→    if test_coverage_actions:
   166→        lanes["test_coverage"] = {
   167→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in test_coverage_actions],
   168→            "file_count": len(
   169→                _files_for_actions(
   170→                    (action for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), files_by_detector
   171→                )
   172→            ),
   173→            "total_impact": round(
   174→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), 1
   175→            ),
   176→            "automation": "manual",
   177→            "run_first": False,
   178→        }
   179→
   180→    return lanes
   181→
   182→
   183→def compute_lanes(
   184→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   185→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   186→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
   188→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→
   190→    cleanup_actions = _cleanup_lane_actions(
   191→        [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
   192→    )
   193→    reorganize_actions = [
   194→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "reorganize"
   195→    ]
   196→    debt_actions = [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "debt_review"]
   197→    refactor_actions = [
   198→        action
   199→        for action in actions
   200→        if action.get("type") not in {"auto_fix", "reorganize", "debt_review"}
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    if cleanup_actions:
   204→        cleanup_files = _files_for_actions(cleanup_actions, files_by_detector)
   205→        lanes["cleanup"] = {
   206→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in cleanup_actions],
   207→            "file_count": len(cleanup_files),
   208→            "total_impact": round(
   209→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in cleanup_actions), 1
   210→            ),
   211→            "automation": "full",
   212→            "run_first": False,
   213→        }
   214→
   215→    if reorganize_actions:
   216→        lanes["restructure"] = {
   217→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in reorganize_actions],
   218→            "file_count": len(
   219→                _files_for_actions(reorganize_actions, files_by_detector)
   220→            ),
   221→            "total_impact": round(
   222→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in reorganize_actions), 1
   223→            ),
   224→            "automation": "manual",
   225→            "run_first": False,
   226→        }
   227→
   228→    if refactor_actions:
   229→        lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
   230→
   231→    if debt_actions:
   232→        lanes["debt_review"] = {
   233→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in debt_actions],
   234→            "file_count": 0,
   235→            "total_impact": 0.0,
   236→            "automation": "manual",
   237→            "run_first": False,
   238→        }
   239→
   240→    if "cleanup" in lanes:
   241→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   242→            if lane_name == "cleanup":
   243→                continue
   244→            lane_files = _files_for_actions(
   245→                (action for action in actions if action["priority"] in lane["actions"]),
   246→                files_by_detector,
   247→            )
   248→            if cleanup_files & lane_files:
   249→                lanes["cleanup"]["run_first"] = True
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    return lanes
   253→
   254→
   255→def _significant_lane(lane_name: str, lane: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   256→    if lane_name == "debt_review" or lane.get("run_first"):
   257→        return False
   258→    return lane.get("file_count", 0) >= 5 or lane.get("total_impact", 0) >= 1.0
   259→
   260→
   261→def compute_strategy_hint(
   262→    fixer_leverage: dict[str, Any],
   263→    lanes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   264→    can_parallelize: bool,
   265→    phase: str,
   266→) -> str:
   267→    """Generate one- or two-sentence execution strategy guidance."""
   268→    recommendation = fixer_leverage.get("recommendation", "none")
   269→    coverage_pct = round(fixer_leverage.get("coverage", 0) * 100)
   270→    lane_count = sum(
   271→        1
   272→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   273→        if lane_name != "debt_review" and not lane.get("run_first")
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    if recommendation == "strong" and can_parallelize:
   277→        return (
   278→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   279→            f"Then {lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   280→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   281→        )
   282→    if recommendation == "strong":
   283→        return (
   284→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   285→            "Then rescan to verify."
   286→        )
   287→    if can_parallelize:
   288→        return (
   289→            f"{lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   290→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   291→        )
   292→    if phase == "maintenance":
   293→        return "Maintenance mode — address new findings as they appear."
   294→    if phase == "stagnation":
   295→        return "Try a different dimension to break the plateau."
   296→    return "Work through actions in priority order. Rescan after each fix to track progress."
   297→
   298→
   299→def compute_strategy(
   300→    findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   301→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   302→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   303→    phase: str,
   304→    lang: str | None,
   305→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   306→    """Orchestrate strategy computation and annotate actions with lanes."""
   307→    files_by_detector = open_files_by_detector(findings)
   308→    fixer_leverage = compute_fixer_leverage(by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   309→    lanes = compute_lanes(actions, files_by_detector)
   310→
   311→    action_lane: dict[int, str] = {}
   312→    for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   313→        for priority in lane["actions"]:
   314→            action_lane[priority] = lane_name
   315→    for action in actions:
   316→        action["lane"] = action_lane.get(action["priority"])
   317→
   318→    significant_non_blocked = [
   319→        (lane_name, lane)
   320→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   321→        if _significant_lane(lane_name, lane)
   322→    ]
   323→    can_parallelize = len(significant_non_blocked) >= 2
   324→
   325→    hint = compute_strategy_hint(fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, phase)
   326→    review_action = next(
   327→        (action for action in actions if action.get("detector") == "review"), None
   328→    )
   329→    if review_action:
   330→        hint += f" Review: {review_action['count']} finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
   331→
   332→    return {
   333→        "fixer_leverage": fixer_leverage,
   334→        "lanes": {name: {**lane} for name, lane in lanes.items()},
   335→        "can_parallelize": can_parallelize,
   336→        "hint": hint,
   337→    }
   338→
```

```
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }

   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open" or f.get("suppressed"):
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   135→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   136→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   137→        )
   138→    return by_detector

   141→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   142→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   143→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   144→    config = {}
   145→    try:
   146→        config = _load_config()
   147→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   148→        config = {}
   149→
   150→    raw_path = default_rel
   151→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   152→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   153→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   154→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   155→
   156→    path = Path(raw_path)
   157→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   158→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   159→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   163→    except ValueError:
   164→        rel_path = str(path)
   165→    return rel_path, path

   170→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT

   173→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   174→
   175→    in_readme = False
   176→    if generated:
   177→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   178→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   179→            if readme_path.exists():

   192→        )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "generated": generated,

   202→def _compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   203→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   204→    if not actions:
   205→        return None
   206→    top = actions[0]
   207→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   208→    if not command:
   209→        return None
   210→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   211→    return {
   212→        "command": command,
   213→        "description": description,
   214→    }

   217→def _compute_why_now(
   218→    phase: str,
   219→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   220→    primary_action: dict | None,
   221→) -> str:
   222→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""

   224→    if hint:
   225→        return hint
   226→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   227→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   228→    phase_default = {
   229→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact findings to establish a clean baseline.",
   230→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   231→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   232→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   233→    }
   234→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open findings first.")

   237→def _compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   238→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   239→    return {
   240→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   241→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   242→    }
   243→
   244→
   245→def _compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   246→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   247→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   248→
   249→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   250→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   251→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   252→    if (
   253→        suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD
   254→        or ignored_count >= 100
   255→    ):
   256→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   257→        message = (
   258→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   259→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   260→            else f"{ignored_count} findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   261→        )
   262→        flags.append(
   263→            {
   264→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   265→                "severity": severity,
   266→                "message": message,
   267→            }
   268→        )
   269→
   270→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   271→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   272→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   273→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   274→        flags.append(
   275→            {
   276→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   277→                "severity": severity,
   278→                "message": (
   279→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   280→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix findings"
   281→                ),
   282→            }
   283→        )
   284→
   285→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   286→    return flags
   287→
   288→
   289→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   290→class NarrativeContext:
   291→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
   292→
   293→    diff: dict | None = None
   294→    lang: str | None = None
   295→    command: str | None = None
   296→    config: dict | None = None
   297→
   298→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   299→    if not lang:
   300→        return raw_history
   301→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   302→
   303→
   304→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   305→    return path_scoped_findings(
   306→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   307→    )
   308→
   309→
   310→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   311→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   312→
   313→
   314→def compute_narrative(
   315→    state: StateModel,
   316→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   317→) -> NarrativeResult:
   318→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   319→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   320→
   321→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   322→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   323→    command = resolved_context.command
   324→    config = resolved_context.config
   325→
   326→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   327→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   328→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   329→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   330→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   331→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   332→
   333→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   334→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   335→
   336→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   337→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   338→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   339→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   340→    action_context = ActionContext(
   341→        by_detector=by_detector,
   342→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   343→        state=state,
   344→        debt=debt,
   345→        lang=lang,
   346→    )
   347→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   348→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   349→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   350→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   351→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   352→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   353→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   354→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   355→    headline = _compute_headline(
   356→        phase,
   357→        dimensions,
   358→        debt,
   359→        milestone,
   360→        diff,
   361→        strict_score,
   362→        overall_score,
   363→        stats,
   364→        history,
   365→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   366→    )
   367→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   368→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   369→    )
   370→
   371→    return {
   372→        "phase": phase,
   373→        "headline": headline,
   374→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   375→        "actions": actions,
   376→        "strategy": strategy,
   377→        "tools": tools,
   378→        "debt": debt,
   379→        "milestone": milestone,
   380→        "primary_action": primary_action,
   381→        "why_now": why_now,
   382→        "verification_step": verification_step,
   383→        "risk_flags": risk_flags,
   384→        "strict_target": strict_target,
   385→        "reminders": reminders,
   386→        "reminder_history": updated_reminder_history,
   387→    }
   388→
```

```
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod

    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang

    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config

    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness

    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    89→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    90→
    91→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    # Load the living plan
    94→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    95→    plan = load_plan()
    96→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    97→    if (
    98→        plan.get("queue_order")
    99→        or plan.get("overrides")
   100→        or plan.get("clusters")
   101→    ):
   102→        plan_data = plan
   103→
   104→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   105→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   106→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   107→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   108→        if active_cluster:
   109→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   110→
   111→    queue = build_work_queue(
   112→        state,
   113→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   114→            tier=tier,
   115→            count=count,
   116→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   117→            scope=scope,
   118→            status=status,
   119→            include_subjective=True,
   120→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   121→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   122→            explain=explain,
   123→            plan=plan_data,
   124→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   125→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   126→        ),
   127→    )
   128→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   131→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   132→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   133→        state,
   134→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   138→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   139→    )
   140→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   141→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   142→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   143→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   144→        state,
   145→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   146→    )
   147→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   148→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   149→    ]
   150→    write_query(payload)
   151→
   152→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   153→    if output_file:
   154→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   155→            output_file,
   156→            payload,
   157→            len(items),
   158→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   159→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   160→        ):
   161→            return
   162→        raise SystemExit(1)
   163→
   164→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   168→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   169→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   170→        return
   171→
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   173→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   174→        state.get("findings", {}),
   175→        state.get("scan_path"),
   176→    )
   177→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   178→    try:
   179→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   180→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   181→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   182→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   183→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   184→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   185→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   186→    )
   187→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   188→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   189→        state,
   190→        dim_scores,
   191→        findings_scoped,
   192→        strict_score=strict_score,
   193→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   194→    )
   195→    print()
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
     2→
     3→Separate module to avoid import cycles — submodules import from here
     4→instead of from __init__.py.

     7→from __future__ import annotations
```
