```
    10→    detector_tools as _detector_tools,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.core.registry import (
    13→    on_detector_registered,
    14→)
    15→
    16→DETECTOR_TOOLS = _detector_tools()
    17→
    18→
    19→def _refresh_detector_tools() -> None:
    20→    """Rebuild DETECTOR_TOOLS from current DETECTORS."""
    21→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.clear()
    22→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.update(_detector_tools())
    23→
    24→
    25→# Auto-refresh when new detectors are registered at runtime.
    26→on_detector_registered(_refresh_detector_tools)
    27→
    28→
    29→# Structural sub-detectors that merge under "structural"
    30→STRUCTURAL_MERGE = {"large", "complexity", "gods", "concerns"}
    31→

    41→
    42→
    43→def _history_strict(entry: dict) -> float | None:
    44→    """Strict score from history entry."""
    45→    return entry.get("strict_score")
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→
    70→
    71→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    72→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    73→    now = utc_now()
    74→    return {
    75→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    76→        "created": now,
    77→        "updated": now,
    78→        "queue_order": [],
    79→        "deferred": [],
    80→        "skipped": {},
    81→        "active_cluster": None,
    82→        "overrides": {},
    83→        "clusters": {},
    84→        "superseded": {},
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    89→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    90→
    91→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    92→    """
    93→    defaults = empty_plan()
    94→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    95→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    96→
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    98→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   100→        plan["deferred"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   102→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):

   106→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   108→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   109→
   110→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   111→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   112→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   113→    if deferred:
   114→        now = utc_now()
   115→        for fid in list(deferred):
   116→            if fid not in skipped:
   117→                skipped[fid] = {
   118→                    "finding_id": fid,
   119→                    "kind": "temporary",
   120→                    "reason": None,
   121→                    "note": None,
   122→                    "attestation": None,
   123→                    "created_at": now,
   124→                    "review_after": None,
   125→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   126→                }
   127→        deferred.clear()
   128→
   129→    # Normalize cluster fields
   130→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   131→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   132→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   133→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   134→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   135→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   136→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   137→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   138→
   139→
   140→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   143→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   145→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   146→
   147→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   148→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
```

```
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""

   241→            "detector": detector,
   242→            "file": "",
   243→        }
   244→        result.append(meta_item)
   245→
   246→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   247→    return result
```

Excellent! Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me create a detailed analysis document for the user:

## COMPREHENSIVE NARRATIVE-CLUSTER INTEGRATION ANALYSIS

Based on my deep read of the narrative module and related systems, here's a complete analysis of how clusters could integrate and where the integration points are:

---

### **1. NARRATIVE ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW**

#### **Core Orchestration: `compute_narrative()` in `core.py` (lines 314-387)**

`compute_narrative()` is the single entry point that builds the complete narrative context from state. It accepts:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NarrativeContext:
    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
    diff: dict | None = None
    lang: str | None = None
    command: str | None = None
    config: dict | None = None
```

**Key finding:** Currently, `NarrativeContext` does NOT accept plan/cluster data. This is the **primary integration point** — it would need to be extended.

**Current data flow (lines 320-370):**
1. Extract `diff`, `lang`, `command`, `config` from context
2. Call `_count_open_by_detector()` → `by_detector: dict[str, int]` (lines 111-138)
3. Build `ActionContext` (lines 340-346) with:
   - `by_detector`: counts per detector
   - `dimension_scores`: from state
   - `state`: full StateModel
   - `debt`: from `_analyze_debt()`
   - `lang`: optional language
4. Call `compute_actions(action_context)` → list of `ActionItem` dicts
5. Call `compute_strategy()` with findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang
6. Return `NarrativeResult` TypedDict containing actions, strategy, tools, dimensions, etc.

---

### **2. ACTION ENGINE: `action_engine.py` (Lines 259-271)**

#### **Current Flow:**

```python
def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)

    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)

    return _assign_priorities(actions)
```

**How it works:**

1. **`_append_auto_fix_actions()`** (lines 91-144):
   - Loops through `DETECTOR_TOOLS` (registry of detectors)
   - For each detector with `action_type == "auto_fix"`:
     - Gets count from `by_detector[detector]`
     - Looks up available fixers from `tool_info["fixers"]`
     - Creates action dict with type, detector, count, description, command, impact, dimension
   - **Limitation:** Works detector-by-detector. Each action targets ONE detector.

2. **`_append_reorganize_actions()`** (lines 147-171):
   - Similar pattern: loops detectors with `action_type == "reorganize"`
   - Appends guidance text from `tool_info["guidance"]`

3. **`_append_refactor_actions()`** (lines 210-222):
   - Handles detectors with `action_type in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}`
   - Special handling for `subjective_review` and `review` detectors

4. **`_assign_priorities()`** (lines 241-256):
   - Sorts actions by type order: `issue_queue(0) → auto_fix(1) → reorganize(2) → refactor(3) → manual_fix(4) → debt_review(5)`
   - Assigns sequential `priority` integers

**Data structures (from `action_models.py` lines 20-33):**

```python
class ActionItem(TypedDict, total=False):
    priority: int
    type: ActionType  # auto_fix|manual_fix|reorganize|refactor|issue_queue|debt_review
    detector: str | None
    count: int
    description: str
    command: str
    impact: float
    dimension: str
    gap: float
    lane: str | None
```

---

### **3. STRATEGY ENGINE: `strategy_engine.py` (Lines 183-338)**

#### **Key Concept: Lane Grouping**

The strategy engine partitions actions into **lanes** — parallelizable work streams:

```python
def compute_lanes(
    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
    
    # Extract actions by type
    cleanup_actions = [a for a in actions if a.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
    reorganize_actions = [a for a in actions if a.get("type") == "reorganize"]
    refactor_actions = [a for a in actions if a.get("type") not in {...}]
    
    # Create lanes: "cleanup", "restructure", "refactor", "test_coverage", "debt_review"
    lanes["cleanup"] = {...}
    lanes["restructure"] = {...}
    lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
```

**Lane structure (lines 205-213, 216-226):**

```python
lanes["cleanup"] = {
    "actions": [1, 2, 3],  # priority numbers
    "file_count": 24,
    "total_impact": 45.3,
    "automation": "full" | "manual",
    "run_first": False,
}
```

**CRITICAL INSIGHT:** The lanes are keyed by **work stream type** (cleanup, restructure, refactor) NOT by cluster. This is the **natural alignment point** for cluster action_types.

---

### **4. CLUSTER SCHEMA: `engine/_plan/schema.py` (Lines 34-43)**

```python
class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    name: Required[str]
    description: str | None
    finding_ids: list[str]
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated
    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key
    action: str | None  # Primary command: "desloppify fix ..." or "desloppify move ..."
    user_modified: bool
```

**Each cluster has:**
- `action`: The primary command (e.g., `"desloppify fix unused-variable"`)
- `finding_ids`: List of findings in the cluster
- `auto`: Whether auto-generated or user-created
- `cluster_key`: For regeneration of auto clusters

---

### **5. CLUSTER → ACTION_TYPE MAPPING: `engine/_work_queue/core.py` (Lines 172-247)**

**Function `_collapse_clusters()` shows exactly how to derive `action_type` from a cluster:**

```python
def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Replace cluster members with single cluster meta-items."""
    
    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
        detector = members[0].get("detector", "")
        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
        
        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action
        if "desloppify fix" in action:
            action_type = "auto_fix"
        elif "desloppify move" in action:
            action_type = "reorganize"
        else:
            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
                action_type = "refactor"
        
        # Build cluster meta-item
        meta_item: dict = {
            "id": cname,
            "kind": "cluster",
            "action_type": action_type,  # ← This is what we need!
            "summary": summary,
            "members": members,
            ...
        }
```

**This is ALREADY DONE in the work queue!** The cluster meta-item knows its `action_type`.

### **6. WHERE NARRATIVE APPEARS: Usage Locations**

#### **A. `next.py` (Lines 61-196)**

```python
def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    # ... setup ...
    
    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    narrative = compute_narrative(
        state,
        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
    )

        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
    )
    write_query(payload)
```

**Narrative goes into:**
1. **`query.json`** (via `write_query()`) — the automation payload
2. **Terminal output** (via `next_render_mod.render_*()` functions)

#### **B. `next_parts/output.py` (Lines 66-112)**

```python
def build_query_payload(
    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    command: str,
    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "command": command,
        "items": serialized,
        "queue": {...},
        "narrative": narrative,  # ← Full narrative object!
    }
    
    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("skipped") or plan.get("clusters")):
        clusters_summary = []
        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
            clusters_summary.append({
                "name": name,
                "description": cluster.get("description"),
                "item_count": len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])),
            })
        payload["plan"] = {
            "active": True,
            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
            "clusters": clusters_summary,
            ...
        }
```

**Key: Plan/cluster info is SEPARATE from narrative!** (lines 90-110)

#### **C. `serialize_item()` in `next_parts/output.py` (Lines 12-63)**

```python
def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    
    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
        return {
            "id": item.get("id"),
            "kind": "cluster",
            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
            "summary": item.get("summary"),
            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
            "members": serialized_members,
            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
            "detector": item.get("detector"),
        }
```

**Cluster items in query.json already have `action_type`!**

### **7. DOES NARRATIVE CURRENTLY RECEIVE PLAN DATA?**

**Answer: NO.**

Looking at `core.py` lines 314-325:

```python
def compute_narrative(
    state: StateModel,
    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
) -> NarrativeResult:
    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()

    diff = resolved_context.diff
    lang = resolved_context.lang
    command = resolved_context.command
    config = resolved_context.config
    
    # ← NO plan data extracted here
```

The `NarrativeContext` dataclass (lines 289-296) has NO `plan` field:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NarrativeContext:
    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
    diff: dict | None = None
    lang: str | None = None
    command: str | None = None
    config: dict | None = None
```

---

### **8. COULD ACTION_ENGINE GENERATE ACTIONS PER CLUSTER INSTEAD OF PER DETECTOR?**

**Yes, with minor modifications.**

Currently:
- Input: `by_detector: dict[str, int]` (e.g., `{"smells": 5, "unused": 3}`)
- Output: One action per detector (e.g., Action(type="auto_fix", detector="smells", count=5), ...)

Alternative for clusters:
- Input: `by_cluster: dict[str, dict]` where each cluster has `finding_ids`, `action`, `description`
- Process: Loop clusters instead of detectors
- Output: One action per cluster (e.g., Action(type="auto_fix", cluster="dead-useeffect-batch", count=7), ...)

**Minimal change approach:**
- Add optional `clusters: dict[str, Cluster] | None = None` to `ActionContext`
- In `compute_actions()`, if clusters are provided:
  - Generate actions from clusters (using `action` field to infer `action_type`)
  - Fall back to detector-based actions for findings not in any cluster
- If no clusters: use current detector-based logic (backward compatible)

---

### **9. DOES STRATEGY ENGINE'S LANE GROUPING ALIGN WITH CLUSTER ACTION_TYPES?**

**YES, perfectly.**

Current lanes are named/typed by action type:
- `"cleanup"` ← contains all `action_type="auto_fix"` actions
- `"restructure"` ← contains all `action_type="reorganize"` actions
- `"refactor"` or `"refactor_0"`, `"refactor_1"` ← contains `action_type="refactor"|"manual_fix"` actions
- `"test_coverage"` ← special case for test coverage refactoring
- `"debt_review"` ← contains `action_type="debt_review"`

**With cluster-based actions:**
- Clusters with `action_type="auto_fix"` → `"cleanup"` lane
- Clusters with `action_type="reorganize"` → `"restructure"` lane
- Clusters with `action_type="refactor"|"manual_fix"` → `"refactor"` lanes
- The same lane computation logic would work unchanged!

---

### **11. MINIMAL INTEGRATION PATH: WHAT WOULD CHANGE?**

#### **Option A: Cluster-Aware Narrative (Minimal)**

1. **Extend `NarrativeContext`:**
   ```python
   @dataclass(frozen=True)
   class NarrativeContext:
       diff: dict | None = None
       lang: str | None = None
       command: str | None = None
       config: dict | None = None
       plan: dict | None = None  # ← ADD THIS
   ```

2. **Extend `ActionContext`:**
   ```python
   @dataclass(frozen=True)
   class ActionContext:
       by_detector: dict[str, int]
       dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
       state: StateModel
       debt: dict[str, float]
       lang: str | None
       clusters: dict[str, dict] | None = None  # ← ADD THIS
   ```

3. **Modify `compute_actions()`:**
   ```python
   def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
       actions: list[ActionItem] = []
       
       # If clusters available, generate from them
       if ctx.clusters:
           _append_cluster_actions(actions, ctx.clusters, ctx.state)
       else:
           # Current detector-based flow
           _append_auto_fix_actions(...)
           _append_reorganize_actions(...)
           _append_refactor_actions(...)
       
       _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
       return _assign_priorities(actions)
   ```

4. **New helper function:**
   ```python
   def _append_cluster_actions(
       actions: list[ActionItem],
       clusters: dict[str, dict],
       state: StateModel,
   ) -> None:
       """Append actions from plan clusters."""
       for name, cluster in clusters.items():
           action_type = _infer_action_type_from_cluster(cluster)
           count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
           
           impact = _estimate_cluster_impact(cluster, state)
           
           actions.append({
               "type": action_type,
               "detector": cluster.get("detector") or "cluster",
               "cluster": name,
               "count": count,
               "description": cluster.get("description") or f"{count} findings",
               "command": cluster.get("action") or "desloppify show ...",
               "impact": impact,
               "dimension": _dimension_for_cluster(cluster, state),
           })
   ```

5. **Update callers:**
   ```python
   # In next.py, status_cmd.py, scan_reporting_analysis.py
   narrative = compute_narrative(
       state,
       context=NarrativeContext(
           lang=lang_name,
           command="next",
           plan=plan_data,  # ← Pass plan here
       ),
   )
   ```

6. **In `core.py`:**
   ```python
   def compute_narrative(
       state: StateModel,
       context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   ) -> NarrativeResult:
       ...
       plan = resolved_context.plan
       
       # Extract clusters if plan provided
       clusters = plan.get("clusters", {}) if plan else None
       
       action_context = ActionContext(
           by_detector=by_detector,
           dimension_scores=dim_scores,
           state=state,
           debt=debt,
           lang=lang,
           clusters=clusters,  # ← Pass clusters here
       )
       actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
       ...
   ```

#### **Changes Required: ~200 lines**
- Add cluster detection helpers: 30 lines
- Modify `compute_actions()`: 20 lines
- Update `NarrativeContext`, `ActionContext`: 5 lines
- Update callers (next.py, status_cmd.py, scan_reporting_analysis.py): 10 lines
- Tests: 50+ lines

#### **Backward Compatibility:**
- If no plan/clusters provided → use detector-based actions (current behavior)
- If clusters provided → use cluster-based actions (new behavior)
- No breaking changes to `NarrativeResult` TypedDict structure

---

### **12. FULL REWRITE: ALTERNATIVE APPROACH**

Instead of clustering detector-by-detector, build actions from clusters directly in the narrative phase itself.

**Pros:**
- Cluster descriptions become narrative headlines naturally
- User-authored cluster intentions flow directly into coaching

**Cons:**
- More extensive refactoring
- Requires new TypedDict for `ClusterActionItem`
- Must handle orphaned findings (not in any cluster)

---

1. **Terminal (scan command):**
   - Headline (phase, dimension insights)
   - Strategy hint
   - Primary action
   - Why now explanation
   - Risk flags
   - Reminders

2. **Terminal (next command):**
   - Queue header with strategy
   - Items listed by group/tier
   - Follow-up nudges

3. **Terminal (status command):**
   - Score summary
   - Strategy hint

4. **query.json (all commands):**
   - Full `narrative` object with all fields
   - Used by LLM agents, dashboards, integrations

5. **SKILL.md (generated):**
   - Uses narrative data for coaching text

### **SUMMARY TABLE: INTEGRATION POINTS**

| Component | Current Input | Current Output | Integration Point | Effort |
|-----------|---------------|-----------------|-------------------|--------|
| `compute_narrative()` | State, NarrativeContext | NarrativeResult dict | Add `plan` to NarrativeContext | Tiny |
| `ActionContext` | by_detector dict | N/A (data object) | Add optional `clusters` field | Tiny |
| `compute_actions()` | ActionContext | List[ActionItem] | Fork on clusters available, call cluster-based helper | Small |
| `_append_cluster_actions()` | Clusters dict | Appends to actions list | NEW function, extract from _work_queue pattern | Small |
| `strategy_engine.compute_lanes()` | Actions list | Dict[lane_name] | No change — works on action_type | Zero |
| `build_query_payload()` | Queue, items, narrative, plan | query.json payload | No change — already handles plan separately | Zero |
| `serialize_item()` | Individual item | Serialized item dict | No change — clusters already serialized | Zero |
| Terminal output | Narrative dict | Rendered text | No change — uses narrative fields | Zero |

---

### **KEY ARCHITECTURAL INSIGHTS**

1. **Narrative is currently detector-centric:** Each action ties to ONE detector. Clusters bundle multiple detectors.

2. **Cluster metadata already exists:** The `_work_queue/core.py` has all the logic to derive `action_type` from a cluster's `action` field.

3. **Lane computation is action-type driven:** The strategy engine naturally partitions by `action_type`, which clusters already carry.

4. **Plan data is separate from narrative:** Currently, plan/cluster info goes into `query.json` as a sibling to narrative, not integrated into narrative actions.

5. **Minimal integration is viable:** Accepting `plan` in `NarrativeContext` and passing `clusters` to `ActionContext` is a 2-field change with backward compatibility.

6. **No breaking changes needed:** Detector-based actions remain available as fallback for findings not in clusters.

This analysis covers all the critical integration points with specific line numbers, data structure details, and a clear path forward.
